# PART 1 — Your unit section(s) of FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 (verbatim)

## Appendix N — Collins 减除法的精确形式 / The precise form of Collins' subtraction method

`\label{app:appN}` · `appN_subtraction.tex` · **24 pp** · appendix (proofs) · written **first** among M/N/O

**Purpose.** Turn "the subtraction step" of every factorization proof into (i) a deliberately elementary conditional theorem (`thm:appN-subtraction`: Collins conditions (CS1)–(CS3) ⇒ remainder power suppressed in an integral norm uniform in $\varepsilon$), (ii) the exact combinatorics that reduce (CS1)(CS2) to grouped power counting (chains, pairing, product formula), (iii) explicit idempotent approximators with their $i0$ and a complete error table, (iv) process-by-process statements with their inputs listed by label (DIS, SIA, thrust, DY, TMD, exclusive), (v) the assembled factorization structure `thm:appN-structure`, and (vi) a complete one-loop example with the exact remainder and a two-loop ladder that shows why grouping is necessary. The numbered inputs (`asm:appN-*`) are exactly the [A]-level content of Collins 2011 ch. 10 and of CSS, now named one by one.

**Depends on.** Ch4 `ir`: `def:ir-regions`, `def:ir-glauber`, `thm:ir-leading-regions`, `prop:ir-power-counting`, `rem:ir-regions-values`, `rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`. Ch9 `method`: `def:method-regions`, `def:method-power-suppressed`, `def:method-neighbourhoods`, `def:method-collins-conditions`, `def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx`, `def:method-subtraction`, `lem:method-ward-multi`, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`, `prop:method-soft-wilson`, `lem:method-soft-unitarity`, `prop:method-fsi` and the statements proved here. Ch10 `disall`, Ch11 `coll` (`lem:coll-final-poles`, `lem:coll-unobserved-jet`), Ch14 `tmd` (`def:tmd-unsub`, `def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-collins`, `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`, `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, `rem:tmd-order-of-limits`), Ch15 `scet` (`def:scet-zero-bin`, pointer), Ch19 `excl` (`def:excl-da`), Ch8 `pdf` (`thm:pdf-moments-local`, `rem:pdf-ff-multren`, `prop:pdf-partonic-dr`). App O (`cor:appO-interface`, `prop:appO-tmd`) for DY. Ch2 `qcd` (`thm:prelim-composite-renorm`), App J (`prop:appJ-local-operators`).

### N.1 `sec:appN-setting` 设定 (2.5 pp)

- **定义 `def:appN-integrand` — 被积函数、幂次参数、围道与测度** (no tag). Units $Q=1$. An **integrand** $\Gamma$ is one graph, or a sum of graphs with a common loop-momentum routing (for cross sections: the cut-summed integrand of `asm:appN-cutsum`), with UV subdivergences removed at the integrand level (`asm:appN-uv`), so that $\Gamma$ is a single function on loop-momentum space, not a formal sum of graphs and counterterm graphs. $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$ is the power parameter (IR regulator over $Q$, e.g. $m/Q$; under `asm:appN-cutsum` also a measured variable such as $\tau^{1/2}$ or $q_T/Q$), $L\equiv\ln(1/\lambda)$; $d=4-2\varepsilon$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$. $\Omega$: a $dL_\Gamma$-dimensional contour (real space or a complex deformation equal to real space at large $\|k\|$), $\mu$ its measure (Jacobian included). $\mathcal V$: finite sums of rational functions of the loop momenta (propagators with their $i0$, eikonal denominators) times measurement functions. Identities in $\mathcal V$ are identities of rational functions times measurement functions, valid almost everywhere on $\Omega$; they involve no integrability. $\mathfrak S(A)$ is `def:method-power-suppressed` (Ch9). `\uses{def:method-power-suppressed}`.
- **引理 `lem:appN-suppressed-sets` — 幂次压低类的基本性质** (数学). $\mathfrak S(A)$ is a linear space; $A'\subset A$ ⇒ $\mathfrak S(A)\subset\mathfrak S(A')$; $\mathfrak S(A_1)\cap\mathfrak S(A_2)\subset\mathfrak S(A_1\cup A_2)$; for a finite cover $\Omega=\bigcup_iA_i$: $F\in\mathfrak S(A_i)\ \forall i$ ⇒ $F\in\mathfrak S(\Omega)$. Remarks to print: why an integral norm (measurement functions such as $\theta(\tau-\tau(X))$ are wrong by $O(1)$ on thin layers — pointwise bounds fail, integral bounds can hold); why uniformity in $\varepsilon$ needs a single integrand (with $\overline{\rm MS}$ counterterm graphs the cancellation is not pointwise and $\int|\Gamma|\sim1/\varepsilon$). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:method-power-suppressed}`. Proof: full (triangle inequality).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-meets`** (Ch9, 数学) — regions are per-line mode assignments $R(j)\in\{S,A,B,H\}$ with $S<A<H$, $S<B<H$, $A\wedge B=S$; vertex mode = supremum of adjacent line modes; pinch sets $\pi_R=\{k: l_j(k)=0\ (R(j)=S),\ l_j(k)\in\mathbb Rn_A\ (R(j)=A),\ l_j(k)\in\mathbb Rn_B\ (R(j)=B)\}$ are affine subspaces; per-line order and meet. Claim: (a) $R'\le R$ ⇒ $\pi_{R'}\subseteq\pi_R$; (b) $\pi_{R\wedge R'}=\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}$; (c) if $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}\ne\emptyset$ and $R\wedge R'\in\mathcal R$ then $R\wedge R'$ is the greatest lower bound of $R,R'$ in $\mathcal R$; if $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}=\emptyset$ they have no common lower bound in $\mathcal R$. *Proof:* full, line by line ($\{0\}\subset\mathbb Rn_A$; $\mathbb Rn_A\cap\mathbb Rn_B=\{0\}$; $H$ imposes nothing).
- **注 `rem:appN-hard-soft-lines` — 区域集合的两条约定** (no tag). (i) Per-line meets can produce non-canonical assignments, e.g. a soft line with both ends at hard vertices; replacing such a line by zero inside $\mathsf H_R$ would create scaleless UV divergences $\int d^dk/k^2$ and violate (CS3), so $T_R$ does **not** replace soft lines with both ends in $\mathsf H_R$ (they are treated as hard; the pinch set is still defined by the labels, `lem:method-meets` unaffected). (ii) Soft lines attached to one jet only are subleading but may stay in $\mathcal R$; with rapidity-regularized TMD approximators they must stay (for bookkeeping of the convention of `rem:appN-spacelike-selfenergy`).
- **引理 `lem:appN-beta` — 邻域指数的可行选择** (数学). With $h(R)$ = length of the longest chain below $R$ in $\mathcal R$ and $h_*=1+\max_{R\ne R_{\max}}h(R)$, the choice $\beta_R=3^{h(R)-h_*}$ ($R\ne R_{\max}$) satisfies $0<\beta_R\le\frac13$ and $R'<R\ne R_{\max}$ ⇒ $\beta_R\ge3\beta_{R'}$. Examples: off-shell triangle $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_{C_i}=\frac13$; two-loop planar vertex $\beta_{(S,S)}=\frac1{81}$, $\beta_{(C_i,S)}=\frac1{27}$, $\beta_{(C_i,C_i)}=\beta_{(H,S)}=\frac19$, $\beta_{(H,C_i)}=\frac13$. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods}`. Proof: full.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-covering`** (Ch9, 数学) — $\bigcup_{R\in\mathcal R}U_R=\Omega$ with $N_R=\{\sigma_{R(j)}(l_j)\le\lambda^{\beta_R}\ \forall R(j)\ne H\}$, $\sigma_A(l)=|l^-|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $\sigma_B(l)=|l^+|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $\sigma_S(l)=\|l\|\equiv|l^+|+|l^-|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $U_R=N_R\setminus\bigcup_{R'<R}N_{R'}$, $N_{R_{\max}}=\Omega$. *Proof:* full ($\mathcal A(k)=\{R:k\in N_R\}\ni R_{\max}$; a minimal element $R_0$ of $\mathcal A(k)$ gives $k\in U_{R_0}$). If a jet cannot be used to deform away from the Glauber region (SIDIS target jet), add for soft lines attached to it the condition $|n_A\cdot l|\ge\lambda^{\beta_R}|\mathbf l_T|$; removed points are assigned to some $U_{R'}$ by this lemma, and `asm:appN-separation` must hold there.
- **例 `ex:appN-vertex-regions` — 两圈平面顶点的九个领头区域**. Inner gluon $k_1$, outer gluon $k_2$: of the 16 mode combinations exactly 9 are leading pinches, $(H,H),(H,C_1),(H,C_2),(C_1,C_1),(C_2,C_2),(H,S),(C_1,S),(C_2,S),(S,S)$ (line modes listed per region; e.g. $(C_1,S)$: $J_1=\{k_1,p_1-k_2,p_1-k_1-k_2\}$, $H=\{p_2+k_1+k_2\}$ plus the photon vertex, $S=\{k_2\}$); the set is closed under per-line meets, e.g. $(C_1,C_1)\wedge(H,S)=(C_1,S)$, $(H,C_1)\wedge(H,C_2)=(H,S)$, $(C_1,C_1)\wedge(C_2,C_2)=(S,S)$; 12 covering relations including the non-obvious $(C_1,C_1)<(H,C_1)$. Only meet closure is checked here, not (GA3)(GA4) or (CS1)–(CS3). `\uses{def:method-regions, lem:method-meets, lem:appN-beta}`. Figure `fig:method-hasse` (owner Ch9). Check: `refdoc/work_R6/check_algebra.py` (Hasse diagram, $\beta_R$, Lemmas on the 9-element poset).

### N.2 `sec:appN-inputs` 几何—解析输入 (2.5 pp)

Notation: $t_R\equiv\lambda^{\beta_<(R)}$, $\beta_<(R)\equiv\max_{R'<R}\beta_{R'}$ (0 if none) — a lower bound on the distance to smaller pinch sets on $U_R$; $s_R\equiv\lambda^{\beta_R}$ — an upper bound on the small components on $U_R$. Names (GA1)–(GA4) replace the reference's (A1)–(A4) (no clash with the tag [A]).

- **假设 `asm:appN-contour` — (GA1) 围道** [\tagA]. There is a contour $\Omega$ (a complex deformation of real space, equal to it as $\|k\|\to\infty$) such that (a) for $\Gamma$ and every $C_R\Gamma$ the homotopy from real space to $\Omega$ crosses no singularity, so the $\Omega$-integral (when it exists) equals the real-space integral defined with each denominator's own $i0$ (as $i\eta$, $\eta\to0^+$); (b) each $\int_\Omega C_R\Gamma\,d\mu$ (defined for $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ by (CS3)) continues to a meromorphic function near $\varepsilon=0$. `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:method-subtraction}`. Status and users: `ASSUMPTIONS.md`; where the fibered densities are absolutely integrable on the real base, (a) is replaced by the graph-by-graph checkable conditions (FR)+(FI) of `thm:appN-fibered-subtraction` (the one-loop triangle: `prop:appN-triangle-cs`); (b) is still needed.
- **定义 `def:appN-meet-closed` — (GA2) 对逐线交封闭的区域集合** (no tag). $\mathcal R$ is meet-closed if $R,R'\in\mathcal R$ and $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}\ne\emptyset$ imply $R\wedge R'\in\mathcal R$. It is a **choice** of $\mathcal R$ (close the leading regions of `thm:ir-leading-regions` under per-line meets), used only in `lem:method-pairing` and `prop:method-product`; `thm:appN-subtraction` does not need it. `\uses{def:method-regions, lem:method-meets}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-envelope` — (GA3) 幂次计数包络** [\tagA]. For every $R_0\in\mathcal R$ there is $\Phi_{R_0}\ge0$ on $U_{R_0}$ with $\int_{U_{R_0}}\Phi_{R_0}\,d\mu\le CL^K$ uniformly in $\varepsilon$, such that (i) every partially approximated integrand of the telescoping sum of `lem:method-telescoping` (each factor $i$ taken as $f_i$, as $T_{R_0}f_i$, or as the envelope $g_i$ of rows 3, 4, 6 of `tab:method-errors`) is $\le C\Phi_{R_0}$ on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$; (ii) on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\ge1\}$ every term of the telescoping sum is uniformly integrable with integral $\le C\lambda^aL^K$ (the error factor itself need not decay); (iii) (pointwise boost bound) on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$, for rows 3, 4, 6: $|f_i-T_{R_0}f_i|\le C(s_{R_0}/t_{R_0})\,g_i$. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-separation` — (GA4) 分离性** [\tagA]. On $U_R$: (i) every line of $\mathsf H_R$ has $|l^2-M_l^2|\ge c\,t_R^2$ (Łojasiewicz-type inequality with exponent 2: "distance $\gtrsim t_R$ from smaller pinch sets" ⇒ "virtuality $\gtrsim t_R^2$"); (ii) every jet line changed by $T_R$ (lines from $\mathsf J_{R,A}$ entering $\mathsf H_R$ that receive projections; lines carrying a soft momentum of $R$) has $|l^+|\ge c\,t_R$ ($B$: $|l^-|$) — $\mathcal R$ contains the (generally subleading) endpoint assignments that make such lines soft; (iii) for every soft momentum $k$ of $R$ and every $A$-jet line $l=p+k$ it flows through ($p$ the $A$-collinear part): $|k^+k^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_T^2$ and $|\tilde D|\ge c\,(|p^+||k^-|+v_A(p))$, with $v_A(p)\equiv|p^+p^-|+\mathbf p_T^2$ and $\tilde D=(p+\pi_-(k))^2-M_l^2+i0=p^2-M_l^2+2p^+k^-+i0$; $B$ jets with $+\leftrightarrow-$. The first inequality of (iii) says that $\Omega\cap U_R$ contains no Glauber region. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:ir-glauber}`.
- **注 `rem:appN-inputs-status` — 各输入的地位** (no tag). (GA2) is a choice. (GA3)(i)(ii) are the integral form of the power counting of Sterman and Collins (`Sterman1978a`, `LibbySterman1978a`, `Collins2011` ch. 5), never written in this integral form at all orders; (GA3)(iii) is the pointwise form of the boost argument, which gives scalings, not pointwise inequalities (where one component vanishes and another does not, "bad suppressed relative to good" has no pointwise meaning), hence the bounds relative to envelopes (`rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`). (GA1) is genuine analytic input: for $e^+e^-$ and DIS it follows from pole-location arguments (`lem:coll-final-poles`, `lem:method-i0-compat`); for DY it fails for single cut graphs and is supplied in the one-sided form by App O (`cor:appO-interface`). (GA4)(i): the exponent 2 is an independent assumption. (GA4)(ii) requires endpoint assignments in $\mathcal R$; in the scalar triangle only the external lines $p_1,p_2$ are changed (automatic). (GA4)(iii)-first = the corrected no-Glauber condition, compatible with `lem:method-i0-compat`: the deformation of one component is limited by the soft line's own pole to $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$, which gives exactly $|k^+k^-|\gtrsim\mathbf k_T^2$; it **replaces** the common claim "$\kappa\sim|\mathbf k_T|$" (OUR-4, misconception M31); the existence of simultaneous deformations of several soft momenta is proven only for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations (`prop:appO-target`). (GA4)(iii)-second: reasonable at finite distance from true pinches in IR-regulated amplitudes, verified in the triangle (§N.7), unproven in general.

### N.3 `sec:appN-theorem` 定理与组合恒等式 (3.5 pp)

$T_R$ ($R\in\mathcal R$) are linear maps on $\mathcal V$ (§N.4); $C_R\Gamma=T_R(\Gamma-\sum_{R'<R}C_{R'}\Gamma)$ (`def:method-subtraction`); $X_{R_0}\equiv\Gamma-\sum_{R<R_0}C_R\Gamma$, $C_{R_0}\Gamma=T_{R_0}X_{R_0}$. The Collins conditions (CS1)–(CS3) are `def:method-collins-conditions` (Ch9): (CS1)$_{R_0}$ $(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$; (CS2)$_{R_0}$ $C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ for all $R\not\le R_0$; (CS3) $\Gamma\in L^1(\Omega)$ with $\int|\Gamma|$ bounded uniformly in $\varepsilon$, and $C_R\Gamma\in L^1(\Omega)$ for all $R$ and $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$.

- **定理 `thm:appN-subtraction` — 减除定理（条件形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）前提：\cref{asm:appN-contour}(a)。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）Let $\Gamma$ be an integrand with region set $\mathcal R$. If (CS1)$_{R_0}$ and (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for every $R_0\in\mathcal R$ and (CS3) holds, then
  $\int_\Omega\big|\Gamma-\sum_{R\in\mathcal R}C_R\Gamma\big|\,d\mu\le C\lambda^aL^K$ for all $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$;
  in particular $\int\Gamma=\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma+O(\lambda^aL^K)$, each $\int C_R\Gamma$ converges absolutely and equals the real-space integral in which every denominator keeps its own $i0$. `\uses{asm:appN-contour, def:method-collins-conditions, def:method-subtraction, lem:method-covering, lem:appN-suppressed-sets}`. Proof: full and deliberately elementary — split $\mathcal R$ into $R<R_0$, $R=R_0$, $R\not\le R_0$; pointwise on $U_{R_0}$: $r\equiv\Gamma-\sum_RC_R\Gamma=(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}-\sum_{R\not\le R_0}C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ by (CS1)(CS2); finite cover (`lem:method-covering`, `lem:appN-suppressed-sets`) ⇒ $r\in\mathfrak S(\Omega)$; (CS3) ⇒ linearity of the integral; the last sentence by (GA1)(a). All content is in (CS1)(CS2). Error type E-N. Content grade [A].
- **定义 `def:appN-fibered` — 纤维化密度、可检验条件 (FR)(FI) 与误差类型 E-N$^\varphi$** (no tag). Light-front components $v^\pm=(v^0\pm v^3)/\sqrt2$; the loop momenta are split into a *base* $b=(k_i^+,\mathbf k_{iT})_i$ and a *fiber* $(k_i^-)_i$. $\mathcal V$ is the space of finite sums of products $\prod_j(a_j(b)+\sum_i\beta_{ji}(b)k_i^--i0w_j)^{-e_j}$ (real polynomials $a_j,\beta_{ji}$; constants $w_j>0$; $e_j\in\mathbb Z$) times measurement functions of $b$; propagators and eikonal factors are affine in the minus components, and the approximators of `def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx` (which zero light-cone components or the IR regulator) map $\mathcal V$ into $\mathcal V$. The **fibered density** $\rho_F(b)$ of $F\in\mathcal V$ is the iterated real-line integral over $k_\ell^-,\dots,k_1^-$ (a fixed order), normalized so that $\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int\prod_id^dk_i/(i\pi^{d/2})^\ell\,F=\int_B\rho_F\,d\mu_\varepsilon$ whenever Fubini applies; each fiber integral is a finite sum of residues. Local conditions (named here; not register entries): **(FR)** (fibered residue admissibility) for a.e. $b$ and at every stage of the iterated fiber integration the current integrand is rational of degree $\le-2$ in the current $k_i^-$, has no pole on the real axis, and has simple poles on at least one side; **(FI)** (fibered–covariant identification) $\int_B\rho_F\,d\mu_\varepsilon$ converges absolutely and equals the covariant value $I_F$ (the Feynman/Cheng–Wu parametric representation of the **whole** integrand, continued in $\varepsilon$). (CS1$^\varphi$)–(CS3$^\varphi$) are the conditions (CS1)–(CS3) of `def:method-collins-conditions` in the norm $\int_U|\rho_F|\,d\mu_\varepsilon$ on the real base. Error type **E-N$^\varphi$**: a bound $\int_A|\rho_F|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C\lambda^aL^K$, uniform in $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$, with the fiber integrated exactly; it implies the integrated statement and is neither E-F nor comparable with E-N on a deformed four-dimensional contour. `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:method-subtraction, def:method-collins-conditions}`.
- **定理 `thm:appN-fibered-subtraction` — 纤维化减除定理** (数学). Let $\Gamma\in\mathcal V$ have region set $\mathcal R$, approximators $T_R$ and Collins terms $C_R\Gamma$ (`def:method-subtraction`), and let $\{U_R\}_{R\in\mathcal R}$ be a finite measurable cover of the base $B$ (no boundedness required). Assume (FR) for $\Gamma$ and every $C_R\Gamma$; for every $R_0$, uniformly in $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ with $a>0$: (CS1$^\varphi$)$_{R_0}$ $\int_{U_{R_0}}|\rho_{(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}}|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C\lambda^aL^K$ and (CS2$^\varphi$)$_{R_0}$ $\int_{U_{R_0}}|\rho_{C_R\Gamma}|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C\lambda^aL^K$ for every $R\not\le R_0$; (CS3$^\varphi$) $\rho_\Gamma\in L^1(B)$ uniformly in $\varepsilon$ and $\rho_{C_R\Gamma}\in L^1(B)$ for each $R$ and $\varepsilon$. Then, uniformly in $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$: (i) $\int_B|\rho_\Gamma-\sum_R\rho_{C_R\Gamma}|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C'\lambda^aL^K$ with $C'=|\mathcal R|(1+|\mathcal R|)C$; (ii) $|\int_B\rho_\Gamma-\sum_R\int_B\rho_{C_R\Gamma}|\le C'\lambda^aL^K$; (iii) if moreover (FI) holds for $\Gamma$ and every $C_R\Gamma$, then $|I_\Gamma-\sum_RI_{C_R\Gamma}|\le C'\lambda^aL^K$. `\uses{def:appN-fibered, def:method-subtraction, def:method-collins-conditions}`. Proof: full — the identity $r=(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}-\sum_{R\not\le R_0}C_R\Gamma$ in $\mathcal V$ (the identity behind `thm:appN-subtraction`) passes to the densities by (FR) and the linearity of the fiber integrals; sum the bounds over the finite cover; (CS3$^\varphi$) for (ii); (FI) for (iii). It replaces, wherever the fibered densities are absolutely integrable on the real base, the unconstructed contour of `asm:appN-contour`(a) by the graph-by-graph checkable (FR)+(FI); `asm:appN-contour`(b) is still needed for `cor:method-removing-eps`. Scope: the triangle (`prop:appN-triangle-cs`), the $\varphi^3_6$ DIS ladder at $x>1$ (`prop:appN-phi36-dis`), IR-finite graphs given (LCE) (`cor:appN-cs3-fibered`); **not** Glauber-pinched cut processes, real-base fibrations with poles (space-like rapidity regulators, `prop:appN-massive-photon`(c)), graphs with $q^+=0$ subloops (pure zero modes in this frame), or IR-divergent graphs (`rem:appN-m1-infrared`). Remarks: the literal (CS3) on the real four-dimensional contour fails already for $\Gamma$ (a $\ln(1/\eta)$ divergence across light cones — why `thm:appN-subtraction` uses a deformed contour; a remark, not an error of any source); for $\varepsilon$-uniform (CS2$^\varphi$) the non-maximal $U_R$ must be UV bounded or carry fast-decaying items; the fibration is part of the statement (adding transverse azimuths to the fiber turns first-order norms into second-order ones, `Collins2011` §10.7.3). Prior art: nested (forest) subtractions at the level of pinch surfaces (`Collins2011` ch. 10; `Erdogan:2014gha`; `Ma2020`); the integral-norm formulation on a real fibered base with the checkable conditions is ours (the proof is the elementary one of `thm:appN-subtraction`).
- **引理 `lem:appN-identification` — 纯分母乘积的纤维化–协变等同** (数学). Let $F=\prod_jD_j^{-e_j}$ with every $e_j\ge1$, $D_j=a_j(b)+\sum_i\beta_{ji}(b)k_i^--i0w_j$, and $F_\eta$ the same with $i0w_j\to i\eta w_j$. Assume (FR)$_\eta$ (for every $\eta\in[0,\eta_0]$ and a.e. $b$: simple poles on the closing side, the same closing sides, no coincident poles), (IL1) $\int d^dk\,|F_\eta|<\infty$ for $\varepsilon$ in an open interval $E$ and every $\eta>0$, and (IL2) the sum $G$ of the moduli of the $\eta=0$ residue terms lies in $L^1(B,d\mu_\varepsilon)$ for $\varepsilon\in E$. Then $\int_B\rho_F\,d\mu_\varepsilon=\lim_{\eta\to0^+}\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int d^dk\,F_\eta/(i\pi^{d/2})$ for $\varepsilon\in E$; with the bridge (IL3) (this limit equals $I_F$), (FI) holds for $F$. `\uses{def:appN-fibered}`. Proof: full — Fubini at $\eta>0$; at each residue stage every other factor becomes affine in the remaining minus variables with $\eta$-independent real part, so $|g_\eta|\ge|g_0|$; the prefactors are $\eta$-independent and the set of contributing poles is fixed by ${\rm sign}(w/\beta)$; hence $|\rho_{F,\eta}|\le G$, and dominated convergence. Scope: not the composite Collins terms (e.g. the $\eta=0$ residue pieces of $C_{C_1}$ of the triangle are not integrable: their moduli grow like $\delta^{-\varepsilon}/\varepsilon$ on $x>\delta$); (IL1), (IL3) are not proven in general; (FI) is proven directly for the four Collins terms of the triangle (`prop:appN-triangle-cs`(b); for $\Gamma$ of the triangle it is the premise of `prop:appN-triangle-cs`(c)) and for the one-rung ladder (`prop:appN-phi36-dis`(c)), so the lemma is a general tool only.
- **引理 `lem:appN-lp-certificate` — 热带/线性规划证书** (数学). Let $f=N/\prod_kD_k$ in local coordinates $y_i=e^{LX_i}$ ($m^2=e^{-L}$, $L=\ln(1/\lambda^2)$) on a polyhedral cell $U$ in normalized log coordinates; $N$ a real polynomial in $(y,m^2)$, whose coefficients may depend on frozen variables in compact intervals of $(0,\infty)$; each $D_k$ comparable on the cell to a positive-coefficient polynomial $g_k$ with explicit constants; measure weights $w$; for each monomial $a$ of $N$: $\psi_a(X)=\langle a+w,X\rangle-a_m-\sum_k\max_{b\in{\rm supp}\,g_k}(\langle b,X\rangle-b_m)$. (a) Every $\psi_a$ has a bounded argmax face $F_a$ on $U$ iff $\psi_{\inf}(r)=\langle a+w,r\rangle-\sum_k\max_b\langle b,r\rangle<0$ for every non-zero $r$ in the recession cone of $U$. (b) Then $\int_U|f|\prod_iy_i^{w_i-1}dy\le CL^d\lambda^{-2\varphi^*}$ with $\varphi^*=\max_a\max_U\psi_a$ and $d=\max_a\dim F_a$ (with polynomial weights $(1+L|X|)^j$: $L^{d+j}$); certified at both endpoints of $[0,\varepsilon_0]$ it holds uniformly in between ($\psi_a$ is affine in $\varepsilon$). (c) *Certificates:* exact rational primal–dual pairs with equal values certify each maximum; the face bound uses only the constraints forced tight by the positive dual variables; the archives store the certified rational functions and are re-verified by independent code. `\uses{def:appN-fibered}`. Proof: full — (a) $\psi_a(x+tr)\ge\psi_a(x)+t\psi_{\inf}(r)$; (b) Borinsky's tropical approximation (`Borinsky:2020rqs` Thms 8.A, 8.B), a Hoffman-type error bound for polyhedral concave functions and a Laplace estimate; (c) linear-programming duality. **Limitations of the verifier (print):** the recession check is exact only for one- and two-dimensional cells (all cells used in this appendix); for $n\ge3$ it is floating-point and is not used; 48 composite "item" records of the log-weighted family are verified at the LP level only. The ingredients are known (`Borinsky:2020rqs`; polytope and region methods `Jantzen2011`, `Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw`, `Ma:2023hrt`, `Arkani-Hamed:2022cqe`); their use to certify Collins' integral norms is ours.
- **引理 `lem:appN-lc-positivity` — 光锥能量分母的定号性（标量三次模型、无质量内线）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Scalar models (M1) (off-shell Sudakov vertex, $d=4-2\varepsilon$, complex "quark" $\psi$ and real "gluon" $\phi$, coupling $g\psi^*\psi\phi$, external legs $p_i^2=-m^2$) and (M2) (DIS forward amplitude in $\varphi^3_6$, $x>1$, every loop carrying target plus momentum); $G$ connected, without tadpoles. For a.e. base point, $\rho_G$ is a finite sum over $x^+$-orderings of $\prod_{\rm lines}(2l^+)^{-1}\prod_{\rm states}(E_{\rm st}-\sum_{l\in{\rm st}}l_T^2/2l^+)^{-1}$ times a positive constant; every energy denominator is strictly negative and all orderings have the same number of states ($2\ell$ for the vertex, $2\ell+1$ for DIS), so $\rho_G$ has a definite sign. Bounds: (M1) $|E_{\rm st}-\Sigma|\ge m^2/2P$; (M2) $\ge m^2/2p^+$ before the absorption and after the emission, $\ge(m^2+Q^2(1-1/x))/2p^+$ after the absorption ($(m^2+Q^2(1+1/x))/2p^+$ for the crossed ordering). `\uses{def:appN-fibered}`. Proof: full — the residue algorithm reproduces light-cone-ordered perturbation theory (`Chang:1973qi`, `Ligterink:1994tm`); no instantaneous terms (scalar vertices) and no zero modes; each denominator is $E_{\rm st}-\Sigma$ with $E_{\rm st}<0\le\Sigma$ (Cauchy–Schwarz after the absorption). Negativity below threshold is classical (analogous to the positivity of the Symanzik $F$ polynomial for space-like invariants); its use for (CS3$^\varphi$) is modest.
- **假设 `asm:appN-lce` — 光锥序表示与协变值的等同（LCE）** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for every graph $G$ of the scalar models (M1), (M2) that satisfies the hypotheses of `lem:appN-lc-positivity` and the infrared-finiteness condition (IRF) — its (renormalized) parametric integral converges absolutely for every $\varepsilon\in[0,\varepsilon_0]$ —, the fibered value equals the covariant value: $\int_B\rho_G\,d\mu_\varepsilon=I_G$. `\uses{def:appN-fibered, lem:appN-lc-positivity}`.
- **推论 `cor:appN-cs3-fibered` — 红外有限图的 (CS3$^\varphi$)** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-lce}。（本推论只涉及部分子态。）Let $G$ satisfy the hypotheses of `lem:appN-lc-positivity` and (IRF). Then (CS3$^\varphi$) holds for $\Gamma=G$: $\int_B|\rho_G|\,d\mu_\varepsilon=|I_G|<\infty$, uniformly in $\varepsilon\in[0,\varepsilon_0]$. `\uses{asm:appN-lce, lem:appN-lc-positivity}`. Proof: full — the definite sign and (LCE) give $\int|\rho_G|=|\int\rho_G|=|I_G|$; (IRF) gives absolute convergence of the positive parametric integrand; exponents affine in $\varepsilon$ bound the integral by its endpoint values. Unconditional cases (neither (LCE) nor (IRF) needed, [R, R_PT]): the one-loop triangle and the one-rung DIS ladder, computed directly (`prop:appN-triangle-cs`, `prop:appN-phi36-dis`). Status of (IRF): proven for those two graphs; **false** for the (M1) graphs of `rem:appN-m1-infrared`; expected from soft power counting for the planar and crossed ladder vertices of (M1) and for all UV-finite (M2) graphs with every loop carrying target plus momentum. **Withdrawn** (X2, final round): "(CS3$^\varphi$) holds for every UV-finite (M1) graph without tadpoles or one-loop two-point subgraphs". Content grade [A] (through (LCE)).
- *Target:* **`prop:method-chains`** (Ch9, algebraic identity, no tag) — chains $c=\{R_1>R_2>\dots>R_j\}$, $T_c\equiv T_{R_1}\cdots T_{R_j}$ (largest leftmost, acts last), $T_\emptyset=1$; $\mathcal O_{\mathcal P}\equiv\sum_{c\subset\mathcal P}(-1)^{|c|}T_c$. Then $C_R\Gamma=\sum_{c:\max c=R}(-1)^{|c|-1}T_c\Gamma$, $X_{R_0}=\mathcal O_{\downarrow R_0}\Gamma$ ($\downarrow R_0=\{X<R_0\}$), $r=\mathcal O_{\mathcal R}\Gamma$; for totally ordered $\mathcal P=\{P_1<\dots<P_n\}$: $\mathcal O_{\mathcal P}=(1-T_{P_n})\cdots(1-T_{P_1})$. *Proof:* full (induction along the order; $(R',c')\mapsto\{R\}\cup c'$ is a bijection onto chains with maximum $R$ and length ≥ 2).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-pairing`** (Ch9, 数学) — pairing decomposition: $\mathcal R$ meet-closed; fix $R_0$; $\uparrow R=\{X>R\}$; $\mathcal I'$ ($\mathcal I''$) = regions incomparable with $R_0$ whose pinch sets meet (do not meet) $\pi_{R_0}$; $R_\wedge\equiv R\wedge R_0$. Pointwise
  $r=\mathcal O_{\uparrow R_0}\big[(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}\big]-\sum_{R\in\mathcal I'}\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}T_R\big[(1-T_{R_\wedge})X_{R_\wedge}\big]-\sum_{R\in\mathcal I''}\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}T_R\Gamma$.
  *Proof:* full (split chains: $\mathcal C_1$ with all elements comparable with $R_0$; $\mathcal C_2',\mathcal C_2''$ according to the smallest element $R^*(c)$ incomparable with $R_0$; elements below $R^*$ are $<R_0$, hence $\le R_\wedge$ by `lem:method-meets`(c); none for $R^*\in\mathcal I''$). Remark to print: expanding the alternating sums $\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}$ regenerates non-integrable terms (`prop:appN-ladder`(c)); the decomposition says which combinations must be estimated, it does not reduce the problem to single approximation errors. Optional figure `fig:appN-chain-pairing` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §4.6).
- *Target:* **`prop:method-product`** (Ch9, 数学) — product formula: assume all pairwise pinch sets intersect, $\mathcal R$ meet-closed, each $T_R$ idempotent, (Com) $T_RT_{R'}=T_{R'}T_R$, (Abs) $T_RT_{R'}=T_RT_{R\wedge R'}T_{R'}$ for incomparable $R,R'$. Then $C_R\Gamma=T_R\prod_{R'<R}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma$, $X_{R_0}=\prod_{R<R_0}(1-T_R)\Gamma$, $r=\prod_{R\in\mathcal R}(1-T_R)\Gamma$; and (CS1)$_{R_0}$ ⇔ $\prod_{R\le R_0}(1-T_R)\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$, (CS2)$_{R_0}$ ⇔ $G_{R_0}(R)\equiv T_R\prod_{R'\in\downarrow R\cup\{R_0\}}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ for $R\not\le R_0$. *Proof:* full — Möbius function of the meet-semilattice: (Com)(Abs) ⇒ $T_{\mathcal S}=T_{\bar{\mathcal S}}$ (meet closure); $\prod(1-T_R)=\sum_{\mathcal K}T_{\mathcal K}\sum_{\mathcal S:\bar{\mathcal S}=\mathcal K}(-1)^{|\mathcal S|}$ over meet-closed $\mathcal K$; with $\mathcal M\subset\mathcal K$ the meet-irreducible elements, $\bar{\mathcal S}=\mathcal K$ iff $\mathcal M\subset\mathcal S\subset\mathcal K$, so the inner sum is $(-1)^{|\mathcal M|}\sum_{\mathcal A\subset\mathcal K\setminus\mathcal M}(-1)^{|\mathcal A|}=0$ unless $\mathcal K=\mathcal M$, i.e. $\mathcal K$ is a chain; for incomparable $R$: $T_R(1-T_{R_\wedge})T_{R_0}=0$. Remark: $(1-T_{R_0})$ supplies the small factor on $U_{R_0}$ (`lem:appN-error-table`); the other $(1-T_{R'})$ supply no smallness but prevent new unregulated pinches when $T_R$ removes IR regulators — why single chain terms $T_c(1-T_{R_0})\Gamma$ are not integrable while $G_{R_0}(R)$ is. Reader check: the 9-element poset with a coordinate-zeroing model (`check_algebra.py`).
- **注 `rem:appN-forest` — 与 BPHZ 森林公式的正确类比** (no tag). The chain expansion resembles Zimmermann's forest formula (`Zimmermann1969`) but differs: forests are families of pairwise nested **or disjoint** renormalization parts, whereas chains correspond only to nested families; incomparable regions never appear in one chain even if their pinch sets intersect, and they interact only through meets (`lem:method-pairing`) — the analogue of overlapping divergences. Under (Com)(Abs) the chain expansion is the product $\prod(1-T_R)$, as the forest formula for a single nested string. As in BPHZ, rigorous estimates exist only for the whole product or suitable groupings, never for expanded single terms (critic O10; misconception M35).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-no-pole`** (Ch9, 数学) — a function meromorphic near $\varepsilon=0$ and bounded on $(0,\varepsilon_0]$ has no pole at 0 ($\varepsilon^kF\to c\ne0$ would contradict boundedness). *Proof:* full.
- *Target:* **`cor:method-removing-eps`** (Ch9, [R, R_cond: `thm:appN-subtraction`, `asm:appN-contour`(b)]) — if $\int\Gamma$ is analytic near $\varepsilon=0$, then $F(\varepsilon)=\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma$ has no pole at $\varepsilon=0$ and $|\int\Gamma-F|_{\varepsilon=0}\le C\lambda^aL^K$. *Proof:* full ($F$ meromorphic by (GA1)(b); on $(0,\varepsilon_0]$, $F=\int\Gamma-\int r$ is bounded by the uniform bound and continuity of $\int\Gamma$; `lem:method-no-pole`; the bound survives $\varepsilon\to0^+$). Meaning: the UV poles of single subtraction terms cancel in the sum over $R$, graph by graph (`prop:appN-triangle-exact`(c)); their organisation into renormalization constants is §N.5 (`thm:disall-renorm-compat`).
- **假设 `asm:appN-values` — 发散链项的取值规则** (no tag: convention). There is a linear rule $T_c\Gamma\mapsto\langle T_c\Gamma\rangle$ (e.g. besides DR, raise every denominator changed by an approximator to the power $1+\delta$, continue in $(\varepsilon,\delta)$, then $\delta\to0$) with **(V1)** $\sum_{c:\max c=R}(-1)^{|c|-1}\langle T_c\Gamma\rangle=\int_\Omega C_R\Gamma\,d\mu$ (compatibility with the absolutely convergent subtraction terms) and **(V2)** scaleless chain terms have value zero. Used only to identify subtraction-term values with term-by-term region-method values ("subtracted jet function = unsubtracted", "hard coefficient = on-shell form factor with IR poles removed"); no rigorous estimate uses it. (V1) is non-trivial because single chain terms are not absolutely convergent for any $\varepsilon$ (`ex:method-naive-induction`(c)), so $\int C_R\Gamma=\sum\pm\int T_c\Gamma$ is not linearity of the integral. Renamed items: the reference's (D1)(D2) are (V1)(V2). `\uses{def:method-subtraction, prop:method-chains}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-collins` — 一般图的 Collins 条件** [\tagA]. For every leading-power integrand $\Gamma$ of the process (a single graph, or a family of graphs with a common routing), including multi-direction processes and non-adapted routing, with its region set $\mathcal R$ (meet-closed, containing the endpoint assignments required by `asm:appN-separation`): (CS1)$_{R_0}$ and (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for every $R_0\in\mathcal R$, and (CS3) holds. `\uses{def:method-collins-conditions, def:appN-meet-closed}`. Full status/users/upgrade path in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`; in the text the 「地位」 field lists what **is** provable: (CS1) for minimal $R_0$ (`lem:method-local-accuracy`); under (Com)(Abs) the reduction to grouped power counting (`prop:method-product`); the one-loop off-shell triangle (fibered (CS1)–(CS3) completely verified, `prop:appN-triangle-cs`) and the $\varphi^3_6$ one-rung DIS ladder (`prop:appN-phi36-dis`); general one-loop Sudakov-type graphs (slot X2b) and the QCD two-rung ladder (slot X2c) were not delivered; the obstacles (term-by-term conditions false: `prop:appN-ladder`(c); (Abs) failure: `rem:method-abs-failure`; non-adapted routing: `rem:appN-routing`); `CollinsSoperSterman1989` §10.1 "there is as yet in the literature no complete and systematic subtraction procedure in QCD… even in the case of deeply inelastic scattering" (survey item 6, [txt]).

### N.4 `sec:appN-approximators` 近似子、误差与 Ward 结构 (3.5 pp)

Recap of the idempotent approximators of Ch9 (`def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx`; no new environment): hard–collinear — in all factors of $\mathsf H_R$, $k_e\to\pi_+(k_e)$ for lines entering from $\mathsf J_{R,A}$ ($\pi_-$ from $\mathsf J_{R,B}$), IR regulators set to zero in $\mathsf H_R$; quark projection $\mathcal P_+=\frac12\gamma^-\gamma^+$ (out-flowing $\mathcal P_-$, since $\overline{\psi_+}=\bar\psi\mathcal P_-$); gluon projector $\mathcal P_A{}^\mu{}_\nu=g_T{}^\mu{}_\nu+n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}$, $H_\mu J^\mu\to H^-J^+-\mathbf H_T\cdot\mathbf J_T$ (longitudinal K term + physical transverse term); soft — $k\to\pi_-(k)$ in all factors of $\mathsf J_{R,A}$, projection $n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}$, $\mathsf S_\mu\mathsf J_A^\mu\to\mathsf S^-\mathsf J_A^+$; factors of $\mathsf S_R$ kept exactly. **No new denominators** are created: the soft-approximated $\tilde D=p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ inherits its $i0$; Wilson-line denominators $1/(n\cdot k\pm i0)$ arise only after the Ward identity; the collinear-PDF prescription is irrelevant ($W[w,+\infty n_B]W[+\infty n_B,0]=W[w,-\infty n_B]W[-\infty n_B,0]=W[w,0]$), the TMD one is not.

- **定义 `def:appN-tmd-approx` — TMD 近似子与快度正规化** (no tag). For $q_T\ll Q$ ($\lambda=q_T/Q$; modes $A$, $B$ collinear, soft $Q(\lambda,\lambda,\lambda)$): $T_R^{(y_A,y_B)}$ as in Ch9 with three changes: (i) in $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\sum\mathbf l_T)$ all collinear and soft transverse momenta are kept exactly (only $\mathsf H_R$ factors are projected); (ii) the soft projection uses the space-like $n_A(y_A)=(1^+,-e^{-2y_A},\mathbf 0_T)$: $\pi_-^{(y_A)}(k)=(n_A(y_A)\cdot k)\,n_B$, projection $n_A(y_A)^\mu n_{B\nu}$ ($B$ side: $n_B(y_B)=(-e^{2y_B},1^-,\mathbf 0_T)$); (iii) in the hard–collinear approximation the $A$-collinear entering momentum is projected by $\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k)=(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)\,n_A$ and the longitudinal term becomes $n_A^\mu n_B(y_B)_\nu$ ($g_T+n_A\otimes n_B(y_B)$ still idempotent) — Wilson-line direction $n_B\to n_B(y_B)$. Light-like limit $y_A\to+\infty$, $y_B\to-\infty$ after combining (pointer to `def:tmd-collins`, Ch14; not a premise of this definition). Relative errors $O(e^{-2(y_A-y_k)})$, $O(e^{-2(y_k-y_B)})$ ($y_k$ the gluon rapidity). Caveats: $\pi_+^{(y_B)}\pi_-^{(y_A)}(k)\ne0$, so (Com)(Abs) hold only to $O(e^{-2|y|})$; the collinear factor of $T_R^{(y_A,y_B)}$ at fixed $x$ differs from the light-front matrix element by $e^{2(y_B-y_k)}$ (`rem:tmd-order-of-limits`). `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx}`.
- **定义 `def:appN-excl-approx` — 遍举近似子与分布振幅投影** (no tag). For a meson jet entering $\mathsf H_R$ through a valence pair with momenta $k$, $P-k$: replace them in $\mathsf H_R$ by $\pi_+(k)=xP$, $\pi_+(P-k)=\bar xP$ and insert the DA projection $\mathsf J_{\alpha\beta}\mapsto\frac14(\gamma^-\gamma_5)_{\alpha\beta}\,{\rm Tr}[\gamma_5\gamma^+\mathsf J]\otimes\frac{\delta_{ij}}{N_c}{\rm Tr}_c[\cdot]$ (idempotent: $\frac14{\rm Tr}[\gamma_5\gamma^+\gamma^-\gamma_5]=1$; projects onto the $\gamma^+\gamma_5$ structure (that of `def:excl-da`, Ch19 — pointer) and onto the colour singlet; $\gamma^-\gamma_5$ is the $\slashed p\gamma_5$ structure since $\slashed p=p^+\gamma^-$); longitudinal gluons with $\mathcal P_A$; soft gluons as in `def:method-soft-approx`. Endpoint region $R_{\rm end}$: one line of the pair soft ($x\to0$ or $\bar x\to0$), $R_{\rm end}<R$. `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx}`.
- **引理 `lem:appN-algebra` — 近似子的代数** (数学). Suppose global loop momenta can be chosen such that for every $R$ and every factor $f$ the momentum substitutions set certain light-cone components of the loop and external momenta to zero (set $Z_R(f)$; "adapted routing"). Then (i) $T_R^2=T_R$; (ii) $T_RT_{R'}=T_{R'}T_R$ and $Z_{RR'}(f)=Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$; (iii) for momentum substitutions and mass zeroing, (Abs) ⇔ $Z_{R\wedge R'}(f)\subseteq Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$ for every factor; with the **soft-first convention** (a jet line carrying soft momentum enters the hard factor with its soft-approximated momentum, `Collins2011` §10.6.6; `prop:appN-twoloop-algebra`) this inclusion holds for per-line meets except possibly in the configuration of `rem:appN-abs-edge`; with the literal convention (hard factors see the exact jet momenta) it fails — in exactly four cases for the two-loop planar vertex (`prop:appN-twoloop-algebra`(b)); (iv) for polarization projections the longitudinal (K) part is absorbed exactly but the transverse $g_T$ part is not: if a direction-flipping line $e$ exists ($R(e)=A$, $R'(e)=B$), $T_RT_{R'}-T_RT_{R\wedge R'}T_{R'}$ is the coupling of the transverse polarization of $e$ at that connection — leading if $e$ is a minimal (physical) connection (gluon-initiated subprocess), suppressed by $\sigma(e)/|e^\pm|$ only inside grouped expressions otherwise. Scalar vertices (triangle) and one-direction processes (DIS) have no flipping lines: (Abs) exact; one-loop graphs, ladders and the two-loop planar vertex have adapted routings. `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, lem:method-meets}`. Proof: full (case analysis: commuting idempotent coordinate/mass zeroings; constant projectors; factor-by-factor check of (iii); $\mathcal P_A$ keeps $V^-W^+$, $\mathcal P_B$/soft keep $V^+W^-$). Reader check: projector algebra with explicit Dirac matrices (`check_algebra.py`).
- **注 `rem:appN-abs-edge` — (Abs) 的一个未被覆盖的情形** (no tag). Configuration: a line $e$ soft in $R$ with both ends at hard vertices of $R$ (so $T_R$ leaves it alone by `rem:appN-hard-soft-lines`(i)), $A$-collinear in $R'$ entering $\mathsf H_{R'}$ at factor $f$ ($T_{R'}$ zeroes only $e^-$, $\mathbf e_T$ in $f$), soft in $R\wedge R'$ with one endpoint in $\mathsf J_{R\wedge R',A}$ (possible if the other endpoint $v$ has lines $(e,g,h)$ of modes $(S,H,H)$ in $R$ and $(A,A,A)$ in $R'$, hence $(S,A,A)$ in $R\wedge R'$): then $T_{R\wedge R'}$ zeroes all components of $e$ in $f$ including $e^+\notin Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$, and (Abs) fails by a momentum-substitution term. Fix: exclude such assignments from $\mathcal R$, or include the case in `asm:appN-collins`. Plausible; slot X2d is **open** after the verification: whether such an $R$ arises as a meet of leading regions (and the version with gluon polarizations) was not checked; the four (Abs) failures of the literal convention (`prop:appN-twoloop-algebra`(b)) are a different, convention-level issue. Fallback in force: the case is part of `asm:appN-collins`.
- **注 `rem:appN-routing` — 没有适配动量分配时** (no tag). In general multi-loop graphs different regions' hard subgraphs have different internal loop momenta; substitutions are then not coordinate zeroings in global variables, and (Com)(Abs) hold only modulo shifts of hard-subgraph loop momenta (integral unchanged, integrand and integral norm changed). `thm:appN-subtraction` needs only (CS1)–(CS3), but `prop:method-product` is not literally usable; the verification of (CS1)(CS2) is then part of `asm:appN-collins`.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-i0-compat`** (Ch9, [R, R_PT]) — a soft momentum $k$ flows through a string of $J_A$ lines $l=p+k$ ($p$ $A$-collinear) along which all $p^+$ have the same sign $\eta=\pm1$ (all final-state: $\eta=+1$), $|k^+|<|p^+|$; deform $k^-\to k^-+i\eta\kappa$ ($\kappa\ge0$). (a) ${\rm Im}\,D=2\eta\kappa(p^++k^+)\ge0$ and ${\rm Im}\,\tilde D=2\eta\kappa p^+\ge0$ (exact, soft-approximated and $M\to0$ denominators never vanish along the deformation, so this part of (GA1)(a) holds for $C_R\Gamma$ built from them); (b) the soft line's own propagator $k^2+i0=2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2+i0$ has ${\rm Im}\,k^2=2\eta\kappa k^+$, non-negative only for $\eta k^+\ge0$; for $\eta k^+<0$ its pole $k^-=(\mathbf k_T^2-i0)/(2k^+)$ lies on the deformation side and the contour must return near ${\rm Re}\,k^-\approx\mathbf k_T^2/(2k^+)$: the achievable amplitude is $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$, **not** $|\mathbf k_T|$, giving only $|k^+k^-|\gtrsim\mathbf k_T^2$; if $k$ also flows through a line with opposite-sign $p^+$ the two denominators require opposite deformations — the Glauber pinch. *Proof:* full (direct computation). Illustration: in the triangle, for $k=(z,\lambda^2/z,\lambda)$, $\lambda^2\ll z\ll1$, the $D_g$ and $D_1$ poles lie on opposite sides, $\sim\lambda^2/z$ apart, and the contour can cross the real axis only in that gap. Pointer: `fig:method-glauber-direction` (Ch9), App O `lem:appO-no-crossing` (two-component deformation).
- **引理 `lem:appN-error-table` — 误差表各行的证明** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}(iii)。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $\beta_R\ge3\beta_<(R)$ (`lem:appN-beta`). On $U_R\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$ the terms deleted by $T_R$ from single factors obey the bounds of `tab:method-errors` (Ch9): rows 1 (hard–collinear line of $\mathsf H_R$: deleted $2(h+\pi_+k_e)\cdot(k_e-\pi_+k_e)+\dots$, relative $s_R/t_R^2$), 2 (masses and external $p^-$: relative $\lambda^2/t_R^2$), 5 (soft, $J_A$ line $p+k$: deleted $2p^-k^+-2\mathbf p_T\cdot\mathbf k_T+k^2$, kept $p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-$, relative $(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}$), 7 (TMD rapidity regularization: relative $e^{-2(y_A-y_k)}+e^{-2(y_k-y_B)}$), 8 (exclusive valence pair, as row 1 with $k_e^+=xP^+$) are pointwise relative bounds $|f-T_Rf|\le\delta|T_Rf|$ using only (GA4); rows 3, 4, 6 (quark projection, gluon projection, soft polarization: absolute $(s_R/t_R)\|\mathsf H\|\mathcal J_+$ resp. $(s_R/t_R)\|\mathsf S\|\mathcal J_+$) are absolute bounds relative to an envelope (= (GA3)(iii)); row 9 (event-shape measurement $\tau(X)-\tau_R(X)$, thin layer) is an integral bound (needs `asm:appN-cutsum`(CU3), applied in `prop:appN-thrust`). Except row 7, all $\delta\le C\big(\frac{s_R}{t_R^2}+\frac{\lambda^2}{t_R^2}+s_R+(\frac{s_R}{t_R})^{1/2}\big)\le C'\lambda^{a_R}$, $a_R=\frac13\beta_R$ ($R_{\max}$: only $\lambda^2/t_R^2$, $a=2-2\beta_<(R_{\max})$); row 7 is estimated only on $Y\ge c_0L$, $|\mathbf k_T|\ge\lambda^{1+a}$ (`prop:appN-tmd-window`). For $\|k\|\ge1$ relative errors need not be small; control there is (GA3)(ii). `\uses{asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-envelope, lem:appN-beta, def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, def:appN-tmd-approx, def:appN-excl-approx}`. Proof: full, row by row — row 1: $l=h+\sum_ec_ek_e$, $\delta_e=k_e-\pi_+(k_e)=(0,k_e^-,\mathbf k_{Te})$, $|{\rm exact}-{\rm kept}|\le2\|l\|\sum\sigma_A(k_e)+C\sum\sigma_A(k_e)^2+M_{\rm IR}^2\le C(s_R+\lambda^2)$, (GA4)(i): exact $\ge ct_R^2$, kept $\ge\frac c2t_R^2$; rows 3, 4: the factor $s_R/t_R$ from the boost argument (bad components carry $|\mathbf k_{Te}|/|k_e^+|$, $\mathsf J^-/\mathsf J^+\sim|k_e^-|/|k_e^+|$ with $|\mathbf k_{Te}|,|k_e^-|\le s_R$, $|k_e^+|\ge ct_R$ by (GA4)(ii)); row 5: with (GA4)(iii) $\mathbf k_T^2\le|k^+||k^-|/c$ and $|\tilde D|\ge c(|p^+||k^-|+v_A(p))$: $|k^2|\le C|k^+||k^-|$, $|2p^-k^+|\le2v_A(p)|k^+|/|p^+|$, and with $\alpha=(|k^+|/|p^+|)^{1/2}$, $2|\mathbf p_T||\mathbf k_T|\le\alpha v_A(p)+\alpha|p^+||k^-|/c$ ⇒ $|\Delta|\le C'(|k^+|/|p^+|)^{1/2}|\tilde D|\le C(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}|\tilde D|$ (no on-shellness needed); row 6 as row 4 with factor $\sigma_A(p)/|p^+|$, absolute because the kept term can vanish (soft gluon with both ends on the same jet: $S^\mu\propto n_A^\mu$, $S^-=0$ — subleading assignments); row 7: $n_A(y_A)\cdot k-n_A\cdot k=-e^{-2y_A}k^+$; row 8: $k\notin N_{R_{\rm end}}$ gives $x\ge ct_R$ by (GA4)(ii); row 9: in the scaling of $R$, $\tau(X)=\tau_R(X)[1+O(s_R)]$, so $\theta(\tau-\tau(X))$ changes only in a layer of width $O(s_R\tau)$; finally $s_R/t_R^2=\lambda^{\beta_R-2\beta_<}\le\lambda^{\beta_R/3}$, $(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}=\lambda^{(\beta_R-\beta_<)/2}\le\lambda^{\beta_R/3}$, $\lambda^2/t_R^2\le\lambda^{4/3}$. Error types: rows 1, 2, 5, 7, 8 E-P; rows 3, 4, 6 E-P relative to an envelope; row 9 E-N.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-telescoping`** (Ch9, 数学) — $(1-T)\prod_if_i=\sum_i\big[\prod_{j<i}Tf_j\big](f_i-Tf_i)\big[\prod_{j>i}f_j\big]$ (projections $\mathcal P$, $1-\mathcal P$ count as factors). *Proof:* full.

- **假设 `asm:appN-ward-product` — Ward 恒等式的乘积结构与减除的重组** [\tagA]. At fixed order, after summing all graphs and all attachments of longitudinal and soft gluons: **(WI1)** each chain term's approximated integrand factorizes by the Ward identities (`lem:method-ward-multi`, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`, `prop:method-soft-wilson`) into "hard ⊗ collinear matrix elements with Wilson lines ⊗ soft matrix element", and the Ward identities apply equally to integrands already modified by other approximators; **(WI2)** grouped by the order of the $A$-collinear part, $\sum_{\rm graphs,R}C_R\Gamma$ reorganizes into the products used in `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`(b) and `thm:appN-structure`. Renamed items: the reference's (W1)(W2) are (WI1)(WI2) here (no clash with (W1)–(W4) of `asm:intro-wightman`). `\uses{lem:method-ward-multi, prop:method-collinear-wilson, prop:method-soft-wilson, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` — 多定域光线算符的 BRST 分类** [\tagC] (new register entry, see `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §00.3; tag fixed at the merge, integrator decision MD1: no all-order argument for the multilocal case exists — Collins 2011 §11.3.2 assumes it, the frontier assessment is [C], `conj:front-brst-multilocal` states the same claim). The BRST-invariant multilocal light-ray operators that appear in the leading-region expansion of Green functions with longitudinal (K) gluon attachments (`Collins2011` eqs. (11.12)–(11.17)) are, modulo BRST-exact (class A) and equation-of-motion (class B) operators, gauge invariant; classes A and B have vanishing matrix elements between physical states and vanishing insertions into Green functions of gauge-invariant operators at separated points. `\uses{def:appM-alien, prop:appJ-local-operators}`. Used only by `thm:appN-structure` (whose content grade is therefore [C]). It is **not** a `\uses` premise of `lem:method-ward-multi` or of the chapter [A] process theorems: there it is a *named gap* (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.6 item 6), stated in their 「地位与缺口」 paragraphs.

### N.5 `sec:appN-processes` 过程 (5 pp)

Opening paragraph: single cut graphs of cross sections have real–virtual cancelling soft and collinear IR divergences; when $\lambda$ is a measured variable rather than an IR regulator, a single cut graph is not absolutely integrable for any $\varepsilon>0$; different cuts have integration manifolds of different dimension and cut lines cannot be deformed — hence a cut-summed representation is needed. Gluon masses for all lines are no alternative (they break the Ward identities in the non-abelian theory).

- **假设 `asm:appN-cutsum` — 割图求和的公共表示** [\tagA]. $\sigma[M]=\sum_G\sum_{\mathcal C}\int\Gamma_{G,\mathcal C}M$ ($G$ forward graphs, $\mathcal C$ final-state cuts compatible with the measurement $M$). **(CU1)** For each $G$ there are a common domain $\Omega_G$ (e.g. light-cone-ordered PT: all minus components integrated by residues for uncut and by $\delta$ functions for cut lines, leaving the $(+,T)$ components) and an integrand $F_G$ (rational functions × measurement) with $\sum_{\mathcal C}\int\Gamma_{G,\mathcal C}M=\int_{\Omega_G}F_G\,d\mu_G$. **(CU2)** The approximators on cut graphs induce linear maps on $F_G$ (commuting with the cut sum: cutting replaces a propagator by $\delta^+$ without changing its momentum; eikonal lines obey the same cutting rules), and (GA1)–(GA4), (CS1)–(CS3) hold for $F_G$, $\lambda$ possibly a measured variable. **(CU3)** (event shapes) for all $R$ and $s\in(0,1)$: $\int_{U_R\cap\{|\tau_R-\tau|\le s\tau\}}|F_G|\,d\mu_G\le C\,s\,L^K$. Renamed items: the reference's (X1)–(X3) are (CU1)–(CU3) (no clash with the verify extensions X1–X3). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:prelim-cut}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-uv` — 被积函数层次的紫外重整化** [\tagA]. **(U1)** $\Gamma$ is renormalized at the integrand level — by DR + $\overline{\rm MS}$ with the subtraction of UV subdivergences organized as local integrand counterterms ($R$ operation in DR), or by Zimmermann's forest formula with BPHZ(L) Taylor subtractions **supplemented by the finite non-invariant counterterms that restore the Slavnov–Taylor identities** (algebraic renormalization; `Breitenlohner:1975hg`, `Breitenlohner:1976te`, `Piguet:1995er`) — so that $\Gamma$ is a single function on $\Omega$ with $\int|\Gamma|$ uniformly bounded near $\varepsilon=0$; graphical Ward identities hold exactly for DR graphs; **(U2)** for every $R$ and every renormalization part $\gamma$, $T_R$ and $t_\gamma$ commute up to polynomials in the external momenta of $\gamma$ (finite local counterterms) that give no leading contribution in the power counting of $T_R$. Corrects ERR-M28 (the passage to $\overline{\rm MS}$ is not merely a finite renormalization of couplings and fields). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, thm:prelim-composite-renorm}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-multren` — 共线因子的乘法重整化（维数正规化路线）** [\tagA]. **(Z1)** In moment space the bare collinear factor renormalizes multiplicatively, $f_N^{\rm bare}=Z_Nf_N^{\rm ren}$ (matrix in parton species, $Z_N$ a pure $\overline{\rm MS}$ pole series, target independent) — a theorem for gauge-invariant local twist-2 operators between on-shell states (`thm:prelim-composite-renorm`, `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`; off shell, mixing with BRST-exact and EOM operators has non-zero matrix elements), an independent input for fragmentation functions (`rem:pdf-ff-multren`: FF moments are not local-operator matrix elements); **(Z3)** (DR route) for on-shell massless partonic targets with DR as the only IR regulator: (i) the factorization identity holds with the same $\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}$ (bare partonic PDFs scaleless, $f^{\rm bare}_{\cdot/j}=\delta$; consistent with `asm:appN-values`(V2) but not derived from it); (ii) $C\equiv\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}Z$ has no pole at $\varepsilon=0$ (mass factorization: the IR poles of the DR partonic forward amplitude are exactly those of $Z^{-1}$; verified at fixed orders by the finiteness of the NNLO/N3LO coefficient functions). (Z3)(ii) is what the process theorems use; under (Z2) it follows from (Z1) and (Z3)(i) by `thm:disall-renorm-compat`. The register's (Z2) is the separate entry `asm:appN-ir-regulator`. `\uses{thm:pdf-moments-local, prop:pdf-partonic-dr}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-ir-regulator` — 保持 Ward 恒等式的部分子靶红外正规化** [\tagC]. **(Z2)** There exist partonic targets $j$ with an IR regularization (parameter $\lambda$) preserving the Ward identities used in `prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`, such that `prop:disall-two-routes`(a)(b) hold and $f^{\rm ren}_{N,\cdot/j}$ is finite at $\varepsilon=0$ and invertible order by order. Status: genuine input — off-shell external partons contradict on-shellness in (Z1) (off-shell PDFs are gauge dependent; BRST/EOM mixing), a gluon mass breaks the non-abelian Ward identities, on-shell massive quarks regulate quark targets but invertibility of $f_{i/j}$ as a matrix needs gluon targets, and no on-shell-gluon regulator preserving the Ward identities and regulating its collinear divergences is known. Partial realizations: non-singlet (quark) targets with massive on-shell quarks; gluon targets only off shell with alien counterterms (`Collins:1994ee`, `Falcioni:2022fdm`), at the price of replacing (Z1) by its off-shell form with BRST-exact and EOM operators. Used only by `thm:disall-renorm-compat` (whose content grade is therefore [C]); the process theorems use the DR route (Z1)(Z3)(ii) and never this entry. `\uses{asm:appN-multren}`.
- *Target:* **`prop:disall-two-routes`** (Ch10, [R, R_cond]) — 从非物理区前向振幅到矩与 $x$ 空间的两条途径: (a) for $|\omega|<1$ ($|x|>1$) the leading regions of $T^{\mu\nu}$ contain only target-collinear and hard modes (no final state; errors by `lem:appN-error-table`; (CS1) for minimal regions by `lem:method-local-accuracy`; the rest by `asm:appN-collins`; `thm:appN-subtraction`; (b) $|r_N|\le\rho^{-N}\sup_{|\omega|=\rho}|\int r(\omega)|$, Taylor coefficients of $T^{\mu\nu}$ = moments of $W$; - *Target:* **`prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`** (Ch10, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-ward-product`]) — in DIS, $R'<R$ ⇒ $\mathsf J_{R'}\supset\mathsf J_R$, $\mathsf H_{R'}\subset\mathsf H_R$, so every $T_{R'}$ changes only factors of $\mathsf H_R$ and the projections between $\mathsf J_R$ and $\mathsf H_R$, never $\mathsf J_R$'s internal factors; with (Com)(Abs) and adapted routing (`lem:appN-algebra`), $\sum_{\rm graphs}C_R\Gamma=\hat{\mathsf H}_R\otimes\mathsf J_R^{\rm bare}$ with $\mathsf J_R^{\rm bare}$ the $d$-dimensional partonic matrix element of the operator of `def:pdf-quark`/`def:pdf-gluon` (no operator renormalization) and $\hat{\mathsf H}_R$ depending only on projected momenta. *Proof:* full (by `prop:method-product`, $C_R\Gamma=T_R\prod_{R'<R}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma$ = subtracted hard factor at projected momenta × exact $\mathsf J_R$; K terms → Wilson lines by (WI1)). - **注 `rem:appN-spacelike-selfenergy` — 类空正规子线的自能** (no tag). For space-like $n_A(y_A)$, $n_A(y_A)\cdot k=-\sqrt2|\mathbf k_T|e^{-y_A}\sinh(y_k-y_A)$ vanishes **inside** the real-gluon phase space; the cut self-energy $\propto n_A^2/[(n_A\cdot k+i0)(n_A\cdot k-i0)]$ behaves like $1/\sinh^2(y_k-y_A)$ — a non-integrable double pole (Wilson-line length divergence), not removable by a principal value (VF-REF-19; c_tmd R4; `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`). It must be excluded, or defined by an explicit convention, consistently in $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}$ and $\tilde S$; any constant $\sigma$ so produced cancels identically in Collins' combination (`lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`): $\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}(y_B)$ contains $+\sigma_B$, the square root contributes $\frac12[\sigma_A+\sigma_n-(\sigma_A+\sigma_B)-(\sigma_n+\sigma_B)]=-\sigma_B$. (Time-like lines give finite constants, $\sigma=1$ in the normalization of VF-TMD-8.) Corrects ERR-M27.

Then $T_R^{(Y)}T_{R'}^{(Y)}=T_{R'}^{(Y)}$, exactly if $p_B^+=0$ and to relative $O(e^{-2Y}p_B^+/p_B^-)$ otherwise; in particular the one-loop $A$-collinear subtraction is $C_A\Gamma=T_A\Gamma-T_S\Gamma$ (same accuracy). `\uses{def:appN-tmd-approx, def:method-subtraction}`. Proof: full — $(p_B+\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k))^2-M^2=2p_B^-(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)$ on shell, while $(\pi_-^{(y_A)}(p_B)+\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k))^2=2(n_A(y_A)\cdot p_B)(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)$ with $n_A(y_A)\cdot p_B=p_B^--e^{-2y_A}p_B^+$; $(g_T+n_A\otimes n_B(y_B))\cdot{\rm soft}={\rm soft}$; if the $B$ lines also carry a $B$-collinear loop momentum $q$, $T_R$ projects $q\to\pi_-(q)$ and $T_{R'}$ keeps $q$ — equal only for on-shell $q$ (the restriction; general case in (WI2)). Reader check: `check_algebra.py`.

- **假设 `asm:appN-endpoint` — 端点条件** [\tagA]. For every graph of the pion form factor (and of the meson side of $\gamma_L^*$ meson production), at each order: **(E1)** endpoint integrability of meson-jet subgraphs — integrating the meson-jet subgraph over its internal loop momenta at fixed fraction $x$ (with absolute values), $|J|(x)$ satisfies $\int_0^t|J|(x)\,dx+\int_{1-t}^1|J|(x)\,dx\le C\,t\,L^K$ for $0<t<1$ (the partonic analogue of "the DA vanishes linearly at the endpoints"); **(E2)** the hard kernel is at most $1/x$, $1/\bar x$ singular at the endpoints; **(E3)** $\int_\Omega|C_{R_{\rm end}}\Gamma|\,d\mu\le C\lambda^aL^K$ and $C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_{\rm end}})$ for every $R>R_{\rm end}$. (E1)(E2) make row 8 hold on $U_R$ and give $O(t)$ bounds for $\int_0^tdx\,|J|(x)/x$; (E3) makes the endpoint region and the leakage of larger regions into it power suppressed. (ii) on $U_S$ the attachment-summed exact graphs are power suppressed — soft regions do not contribute at leading power; (iii) endpoint contributions are power suppressed; (iv) `thm:appN-subtraction` gives factorization with only hard–collinear subtraction terms; (b) $\gamma_L^*$ meson production: as (a) on the meson side; the proton side is a single forward jet (non-forward matrix element → GPD), soft lines attached to it belong to it, Glauber excluded by (GA4)(iii) — an assumption for DVMP; (c) if the hard kernel is more singular than $1/x$ at an endpoint (twist-3 contributions, transverse photons) so that $\int_0dx\,|J|(x)|\mathsf H(x)|$ diverges, (E2) and (GA3) fail on $U_{R_{\rm end}}$: the premise of `thm:appN-subtraction` is violated. *Proof plan:* full given the premises — $i0$: no final-state cut; this holds for every integrand $T_R$ acts on, hence for all chain terms with maximum $R$, hence for $\sum C_R\Gamma$; (ii) the same for $T_S\Gamma$: $\sum T_S\Gamma=0$, so $\sum\Gamma=\sum(1-T_S)\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_S)$ by `lem:method-local-accuracy` — "the dipole cancellation is the smallness of the approximation error, not of single graphs"; (iii) (E3); (iv) `thm:appN-subtraction`; (c) no integrable envelope.
