- **定理 `thm:appN-structure` — 由减除法得到的因子化结构（条件形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-contour}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-uv}、\cref{asm:appN-multren}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}、\cref{asm:appN-brst-nonlocal}，以及各过程所列者：(a) DIS 与 $e^+e^-\to hX$——\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}（$x$ 空间与 SIA）；(b) 推力——\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-values}；(c) DY——\cref{cor:appO-interface}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}；(d) TMD——\cref{asm:appN-tmd}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}（DY 另需 \cref{prop:appO-tmd}）；(e) 遍举——\cref{asm:appN-endpoint}。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）At each order in $\alpha_s$, at leading power, with error $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ for partonic integrands: (a) DIS (moments need complex-$\omega$ uniformity, `prop:disall-two-routes`) and $e^+e^-\to hX$ (`prop:appN-sia`) have the collinear-factorized structure with hard coefficients $C=\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}Z$, finite by `asm:appN-multren`(Z3)(ii) and given order by order by the DR recursion $C^{[n]}=[T^{\rm DR}_{(j)}Z]^{[n]}$ (on-shell massless partonic targets $j$); (b) thrust (`prop:appN-thrust`): $H=|C|^2$, $C^{[n]}$ = $n$-th order on-shell massless $q\bar q$ form factor minus all its collinear and soft subregion subtraction terms, which by (V2) are scaleless, so $C$ is the on-shell form factor with IR poles removed; (c) DY (`prop:appN-dy`): collinear structure with the DIS PDFs; hard coefficients by the same recursion with two partonic targets; (d) TMD (`prop:appN-tmd-window`, `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`, `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`): for $q_T\ll Q$ Collins' structure with $\tilde f_{A,B}$ given by the square-root construction in the limit $y_A\to\infty$, $y_B\to-\infty$; $H^{[n]}$ = $n$-th order leading-power partonic cross section minus $\sum_{m<n}H^{[m]}[\tilde f_A\tilde f_B]^{[n-m]}_{\rm partonic}$ (its identification with the modulus squared of the on-shell form factor with IR poles removed requires the TMD analogue of (Z3) — not verified for TMDs); $q_T\sim Q$ and $q_T\sim\Lambda$ not covered; (e) exclusive (`prop:excl-soft-cancel`): $T_H^{[n]}$ = partonic amplitude (on-shell collinear $q\bar q$ in/out) minus $\sum\phi^{[k]}\otimes T_H^{[m]}\otimes\phi^{[l]}$ ($k+m+l=n$, $m<n$). `\uses{asm:appN-contour, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-uv, asm:appN-multren, asm:appN-ward-product, asm:appN-brst-nonlocal, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-values, asm:appN-tmd, asm:appN-endpoint, cor:appO-interface, prop:appO-tmd, thm:appN-subtraction, prop:disall-two-routes, prop:disall-factorized-subtraction, prop:appN-sia, prop:appN-thrust, prop:appN-dy, prop:appN-tmd-window, prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft, lem:tmd-sqrt-identity, prop:excl-soft-cancel, lem:method-soft-unitarity}`. Proof: full deduction — `thm:appN-subtraction` per graph (or cut-summed integrand); sum graphs; DIS by `prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`, others by (WI1)(WI2) (with `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` behind `lem:method-ward-multi`): each $C_R$ summed over graphs = subtracted hard ⊗ bare factors (minus zero-bins); regroup — fix the hard part and sum jet/soft graphs → full operator matrix elements; fix the factors and sum hard graphs with recursive subtractions → target-independent coefficients; soft factor 1 by unitarity (inclusive collinear, `lem:method-soft-unitarity`), $S_T$ for thrust, split between the TMDs, zero for exclusive; (Z1) and (Z3)(ii) of `asm:appN-multren` ((T3) for TMD) replace bare by renormalized factors (the regulator route `thm:disall-renorm-compat` is a consistency check under (Z2), not a premise). Content grade **[C]**, through the named gap `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` (all other premises are \tagA; without that entry the content grade would be [A], the weakest premises being `asm:appN-collins` and the other \tagA inputs). The chapter [A] process theorems do not use this theorem as a premise (decision MD1). This is the precise form of the deduction inside `thm:method-template`'s `proofsketch` (Ch9 uses it as a forward-to-appendix premise, allowed).

### N.7 `sec:appN-examples` 例子 (3.5 pp)

Setting of the one-loop off-shell triangle (used by `ex:method-naive-induction`, `ex:method-sudakov-subtraction`, Ch5 `ex:sudakov-offshell`): scalar triangle with massless internal lines; measure $\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int d^dk/(i\pi^{d/2})$; $p_1=(P,-\frac{m^2}{2P},\mathbf 0_T)$, $p_2=(\frac{m^2}{2P'},-P',\mathbf 0_T)$, $p_i^2=-m^2$; $\tilde Q^2\equiv2PP'$, $Q^2\equiv-(p_1+p_2)^2=(\tilde Q^2+m^2)^2/\tilde Q^2=\tilde Q^2+2m^2+m^4/\tilde Q^2$; $D_g=-k^2$, $D_1=-(p_1-k)^2=m^2+2p_1\cdot k-k^2$, $D_2=-(p_2+k)^2=m^2-2p_2\cdot k-k^2$ (all with $-i0$), $\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1D_2)$; $e_1=m^2+2Pk^-$, $h_1=2Pk^-$, $t_1=2Pk^--k^2$; $e_2=m^2+2P'k^+$, $h_2=2P'k^+$, $t_2=2P'k^+-k^2$; $T_S:(D_1,D_2)\mapsto(e_1,e_2)$, $T_H:(D_1,D_2)\mapsto(t_1,t_2)$, $T_{C_1}:D_2\mapsto h_2$, $T_{C_2}:D_1\mapsto h_1$ (each also acting on already approximated denominators: $T_He_i=h_i$, $T_St_i=h_i$, $T_{C_1}e_2=h_2$, …; $h_i-t_i=k^2=-D_g$); $\mathcal R=\{S<C_1,C_2<H\}$, $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_C=\frac13$.

- **命题 `prop:appN-triangle-exact` — 单圈离壳三角图：Collins 减除的完整计算与精确余项** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$：在重整化微扰论中对红外正规化的部分子态严格。）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）In the setting above:
  (a) *(closed forms)* On each quark line the approximations form the four-element semilattice $\{1,e,t,h\}$ ($e\circ t=t\circ e=h$, $h\circ x=h$, $x\circ x=x$), so (Com) and (Abs) hold exactly and, by `prop:method-product`, $C_S\Gamma=\frac1{D_ge_1e_2}$, $C_{C_1}\Gamma=\frac1{D_gh_2}\big(\frac1{D_1}-\frac1{e_1}\big)$, $C_{C_2}\Gamma=\frac1{D_gh_1}\big(\frac1{D_2}-\frac1{e_2}\big)$, $C_H\Gamma=\frac1{D_g}\big(\frac1{t_1}-\frac1{h_1}\big)\big(\frac1{t_2}-\frac1{h_2}\big)=\frac{D_g}{t_1h_1t_2h_2}$, and $r=\frac1{D_g}\big[\Delta_1\Delta_2-\Delta_1'\Delta_2'-\delta_1\delta_2\big]$ with $\Delta_i=\frac1{D_i}-\frac1{h_i}$, $\Delta_i'=\frac1{e_i}-\frac1{h_i}$, $\delta_i=\frac1{t_i}-\frac1{h_i}$;
  (b) *(subtraction terms as absolutely convergent iterated integrals)* after the $k^-$ integral by residues, every $C_R\Gamma$ is an absolutely convergent $(k^+,\mathbf k_T)$ integral for $0<\varepsilon<1$; e.g. for $0<k^+<P$: $\int\frac{dk^-}{2\pi}C_{C_1}\Gamma=\frac i2\frac{m^2k^+/(2PP')}{[k^+m^2(1-k^+/P)+P\mathbf k_T^2][k^+m^2+P\mathbf k_T^2]}$ (vanishing linearly as $k^+\to0$ while each of its two terms diverges like $1/k^+$); for $k^+>P$ only the tail of $-T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$, $\propto-1/[k^+(k^+m^2+P\mathbf k_T^2)]$; for $k^+<0$ zero. Values: $\int C_H\Gamma=\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/\tilde Q^2)^\varepsilon\mathcal A$, $\int C_{C_1}\Gamma=\int C_{C_2}\Gamma=-\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/m^2)^\varepsilon\mathcal A$, $\int C_S\Gamma=\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2\tilde Q^2/m^4)^\varepsilon\mathcal B$, with $\mathcal A=\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)^2}{\varepsilon^2\Gamma(1-2\varepsilon)}=-\Gamma(\varepsilon)J(\varepsilon)$, $J(\varepsilon)=\frac{\Gamma(-\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)}{\Gamma(1-2\varepsilon)}$, $\mathcal B=\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)^2\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^2}$ (the $C_S$ $k^+$ integral is a Beta function, $\int_0^\infty dk^+(k^+)^{-\varepsilon}/(m^2+2P'k^+)=(m^2)^{-\varepsilon}(2P')^{\varepsilon-1}\Gamma(\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)$, not $J$);
  (c) *(sum)* the poles cancel ($\varepsilon^{-2}$: $1-2+1=0$; $\varepsilon^{-1}$: $\ell_Q-2\ell_m+(2\ell_m-\ell_Q)=0$, $\ell_Q=\ln\frac{\mu^2}{\tilde Q^2}$, $\ell_m=\ln\frac{\mu^2}{m^2}$) and $\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma\big|_{\varepsilon\to0}=\tilde Q^{-2}\big[\ln^2\frac{\tilde Q^2}{m^2}+\frac{\pi^2}3\big]$;
  (d) *(exact remainder)* with $I=\Phi^{(1)}(t,t)/Q^2$, $t=m^2/Q^2$, $L=\ln(Q^2/m^2)$:
  $\int r=-\frac{4m^2}{Q^4}+\frac{m^4}{Q^6}\Big(L^2-15+\frac{\pi^2}3\Big)+O\Big(\frac{m^6}{Q^8}L^2\Big)$ —
  a full power $\lambda^2$ with **no logarithm at next-to-leading power**: the logarithms of the NLP of $I$, $t(2L^2-4L-4+\frac{2\pi^2}3)$, are reproduced by the subtraction terms through the exact kinematics $\tilde Q^2\ne Q^2$; by contrast the general argument (`lem:appN-error-table`) guarantees only relative errors $O(\lambda^{1/9})$ on $U_S$ here;
  (e) *(values and the convention)* in the light-cone representation the zero-bin $T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$ is the formal expression $\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon\tilde Q^2}(\frac{\mu^2}{m^2})^\varepsilon\int_0^\infty\frac{d\sigma_1}{\sigma_1^{1+\varepsilon}}=(\dots)\big(\frac1{\varepsilon_{\rm UV}}-\frac1{\varepsilon_{\rm IR}}\big)$ ($\sigma_1=k^+/P$; divergent for every $\varepsilon$), to which (V2) assigns 0; (V1) holds: the Feynman-parameter continuation of $T_{C_1}\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1h_2)$, $\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int_0^1dx\,x^{-1-\varepsilon}\int_0^\infty d\sigma[(1-x)m^2+\sigma\tilde Q^2]^{-1-\varepsilon}=\Gamma(\varepsilon)J(\varepsilon)\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/m^2)^\varepsilon$, equals the absolutely convergent $\int C_{C_1}\Gamma$; a parametric soft expansion over $[0,1]$ would instead give $-1/\varepsilon$ (representation dependence, `rem:ir-regions-values`); a direct Feynman parametrization of $1/(D_ge_1h_2)$ gives $\propto B(-\varepsilon,\varepsilon)=0$.
  **Status sentence (print verbatim in substance):** 「在 $k^-$ 留数表示中，(CS1)–(CS3) 对本例已完整验证（\cref{prop:appN-triangle-cs}）；四个减除项的纤维化值与协变值精确相同」 (the corresponding identity for $\Gamma$ is the premise of `prop:appN-triangle-cs`(c) and is not asserted in print; X2 §11 gives the sentence to use once it is accepted; verification bookkeeping is never printed, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.12); the identities, the values and the remainder are exact. The negative-$k^+$ sliver ($-m^2/2P'<k^+<0$, in this routing and fibration) carries $\zeta_2/\tilde Q^2$ of $\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma$ exactly and $-2\lambda^2$ of the remainder (elementary). `\uses{prop:method-product, lem:appN-algebra, asm:appN-values}` — note: `asm:appN-values` is cited in (e) only to name the convention; the rigorous content of (e) is the computation, so the writer may treat it as a pointer (report it). Proof: full — closed forms by `prop:method-product`; $k^-$ residues (close the contour; for fixed $(k^+,\mathbf k_T)$ each term decays like $|k^-|^{-2}$); $\int d^{2-2\varepsilon}k_T(a+bk_T^2)^{-1}=\pi^{1-\varepsilon}\Gamma(\varepsilon)a^{-\varepsilon}b^{\varepsilon-1}$ (partial fractions first for $C_H$); $k^+$ integrals → $J(\varepsilon)$ for $C_{C_i},C_H$ (the first integral of $J$ is analytic for ${\rm Re}\,\varepsilon<1$) and a Beta function for $C_S$; UV ends need $\varepsilon>0$, the endpoint $k^+\to P$ needs $\varepsilon<1$; (d) from the closed form $Q^2I=\Phi^{(1)}(t,t)=\frac{4{\rm Li}_2(-\rho t)+\ln^2(\rho t)+\pi^2/3}{\sqrt{1-4t}}$, $\rho=\frac2{1-2t+\sqrt{1-4t}}$ (`UsyukinaDavydychev1993a`), expanded as $L^2+\frac{\pi^2}3+t(2L^2-4L-4+\frac{2\pi^2}3)+t^2(6L^2-14L-11+2\pi^2)+O(t^3L^2)$, minus (c) written with $\tilde Q^2=Q^2[1-2t-\dots]$ — a Table-5-type comparison of $I$ with $L^2+\pi^2/3$ in $Q^2$ does **not** test the remainder (ERR-m25). Checks: `refdoc/work_R6/check_remainder_d4.py` (the $d=4$ remainder density $\tilde r(k^+,\mathbf k_T^2)=\rho_\Gamma-\sum_R\rho_{C_R}$ integrates to $-0.0173434339581$ at $m^2=0.02$, $P=0.8$, $P'=1.3$, 12 digits); `check_algebra.py` (50/50); `check_triangle_numerics.py` (values (b) at $\varepsilon=0.3$, $P=0.8$, $P'=1.3$, $m^2=0.1$, $\mu=1$: $C_{C_1}$ $-7.26621198874$ vs $-7.26621198785$; $C_S$ $27.6979046630$ vs $27.6979046621$; $C_H$ $2.92340552296$ vs $2.92340552583$); `j07.py` ($J(\varepsilon)$ to 25 digits); `refdoc/work_SYN/triangle_remainder.py` (closed form vs 1D integral $<10^{-49}$; remainder $Q^2\int r/t\to-4$; $t^2$ coefficient residuals $O(tL^2)$); `spec_v2/checks/fig_data.py` (figure data). VF-REF-2, VF-REF-3, VF-REF-18. Figures `fig:appN-triangle-regions` (N20), `fig:appN-triangle-remainder` (N21; tested starter code in FIGURES_INDEX_v2). Error type of (d): exact. Sharpened result, not marked new (S59; N12 confirmed).
- *Target details for* **`ex:method-naive-induction`** (Ch9, [R, R_PT]): (a) $T_{C_2}\Gamma=1/(D_gh_1D_2)$ has an unregulated massless collinear pinch in $C_1$ (the $D_g$ pole $k^-=(\mathbf k_T^2-i0)/(2k^+)$ below, the $h_1$ pole $k^-=+i0$ above, pinching at $k^-=\mathbf k_T=0$, $k^+\sim Q$; $\int d^{2-2\varepsilon}k_T/\mathbf k_T^2$ converges only for $\varepsilon<0$), while $C_{C_2}\Gamma=T_{C_2}(\Gamma-T_S\Gamma)=\frac{m^2}{2PP'}\frac1{D_gD_2e_2}-\frac1{h_1D_2e_2}$ (using $e_2-D_2=(m^2/P')k^--D_g$; each term contains only one of the two pinching poles) is suppressed; (b) $C_S\Gamma=1/(D_ge_1e_2)\sim\lambda^{-4}Q^{-6}$ is leading in $C_1$, so $T_{C_1}$ must be accurate on $T_S\Gamma$: $(1-T_{C_1})T_S\Gamma=\frac1{D_ge_1}\big(\frac1{e_2}-\frac1{h_2}\big)$, relatively $O(m^2/(Qk^+))$; (c) $T_{C_1}\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1h_2)$ is not absolutely integrable for any $\varepsilon$: at $k=(a^2\kappa^+,\kappa^-,a\boldsymbol\kappa_T)$, $a\to0$ (gluon collinear to $p_2$; rapidity end, genuine pinch of the $h_2$ pole $k^+=+i0$ with the $D_g$ pole) the integrand is $\sim a^{-4}$, the measure $\sim a^{4-2\varepsilon}$ — needs $\varepsilon<0$; in the UV direction $k=(\kappa^+,a\kappa^-,\sqrt a\boldsymbol\kappa_T)$, $a\to\infty$ — needs $\varepsilon>0$; the same for the scaleless $T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$; $T_H\Gamma$ converges only for $\varepsilon<0$. VF-REF-18. Lessons (print): accuracy on $X_{R_0}$ ⇒ (CS1); suppression of whole subtraction terms ⇒ (CS2); integrability only of grouped expressions ⇒ (CS3), (GA1); $\lambda$-dependent neighbourhoods; contour; UV. Misconception M35.
- *Target details for* **`ex:method-sudakov-subtraction`** (Ch9): the off-shell triangle in light-cone variables — $k^-$ residues; the four regions with $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_C=\frac13$; which grouped expression supplies the small factor on each $U_R$: on $U_H$ the (CS1) object $r=\frac1{D_g}[(\Delta_1-\delta_1)\Delta_2+\delta_1(\Delta_2-\delta_2)-\Delta'_1\Delta'_2]$ with $\Delta_i-\delta_i=(t_i-D_i)/(D_it_i)$ and $\Delta'_i=-m^2/(e_ih_i)$, both $O(m^2/t_H^2)=O(\lambda^{4/3})$ relative to $1/D_i$; at $\|k\|\to\infty$, $r\sim m^2/\|k\|^6$; on $U_{C_1}$ ($|k^+|\ge\lambda^{1/9}/2$, $|k^-|,|\mathbf k_T|\le\lambda^{1/3}$) the (CS1) object $(1-T_{C_1})(1-T_S)\Gamma=\frac1{D_g}[\frac1{D_1}\Delta_2-\frac1{e_1}\Delta'_2]$, small factors relatively $O(\lambda^{2-1/9}+\lambda^{1/3})$, and the (CS2) objects $C_{C_2}\Gamma$ ($O(\lambda^{2/3}L)$ over the box, needs the $k^-$ contour away from 0 by a power of $\lambda$) and $C_H\Gamma$; on $U_S$ the (CS1) object $(1-T_S)\Gamma$ with relative error $\le C|k^+|/P\le C\lambda^{1/9}$ on a contour with (GA4)(iii) (here $v_A(p_1)=m^2/2$); **the soft approximation holds only on a contour that crosses the real axis near the gluon pole, ${\rm Re}\,k^-\sim\mathbf k_T^2/(4k^+)$, and fails at $k^-\approx0$** (there $|e_1|\approx m^2$ against the deleted $\mathbf k_T^2\approx m^2$) — the simplest instance of "the approximation holds only on the deformed contour"; the values, the pole cancellation, (c) and the exact remainder of `prop:appN-triangle-exact`; the zero-bin and its representation dependence; figure `fig:method-sudakov-plane` (Ch9). The existing DR on-shell paragraph is its last part.
- **命题 `prop:appN-ladder` — 两级梯形图：嵌套区域与余项的乘积结构** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）DIS forward ladder (two rungs), external quark off shell ($p^2=-m^2$), internal lines massless; routing $(k_1,k_2)$; $\Gamma=\mathsf H_0(k_2)K_2(k_2,k_1)K_1(k_1,p)$ ($\mathsf H_0$ the top quark line with both photon vertices; $K_2$, $K_1$ the upper and lower rungs). Regions form the chain $R_2<R_1<R_H$ ($R_2$: both rungs collinear; $R_1$: only the lower rung collinear; $R_H$: all hard); $T_2$: in $\mathsf H_0$, $k_2\to\pi_+k_2$ and the Dirac projection $\mathcal P$; $T_1$: in $\mathsf H_0$ and $K_2$, $k_1\to\pi_+k_1$ and $\mathcal P$; $T_H$: $p\to\hat p=\pi_+(p)$, $m\to0$; (Com) holds (`lem:appN-algebra`). With $\Delta\mathsf H_0\equiv\mathsf H_0(k_2)-\mathsf H_0(\pi_+k_2)\mathcal P$, $\Delta K_2\equiv K_2(k_2,k_1)-K_2(k_2,\pi_+k_1)\mathcal P$, $\Delta K_1\equiv K_1(k_1,p)-K_1(k_1,\hat p)$: (a) $r=(1-T_H)(1-T_1)(1-T_2)\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0\,\Delta K_2\,\Delta K_1$; $C_2\Gamma=[\mathsf H_0(\pi_+k_2)\mathcal P]K_2K_1$, $C_1\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0[K_2(k_2,\pi_+k_1)\mathcal P]K_1$, $C_H\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0\,\Delta K_2\,K_1(k_1,\hat p)$; (b) $C_1\Gamma$ integrated over $k_2$ is the one-loop hard coefficient $\hat{\mathsf H}^{[1]}(k_1^+)$ (unsubtracted one-loop hard part minus tree ⊗ one-loop partonic collinear factor), $C_H\Gamma$ gives the two-loop coefficient, and expanding $\Delta\mathsf H_0\Delta K_2$ produces the two subtractions $\hat{\mathsf H}^{[1]}\otimes f^{[1]}$ and $\mathsf H^{[0]}\otimes f^{[2]}$ — the integrand form of `thm:disall-renorm-compat`(c) at $n=2$; (c) the single chain term $T_H(1-T_2)\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0K_2(k_2,k_1)K_1(k_1,\hat p)$ is not in $L^1(U_{R_2})$ (a massless collinear singularity at $k_1\parallel\hat p$, convergent only for $\varepsilon<0$, where $\Delta\mathsf H_0K_2$ does not vanish), whereas the grouped $C_H\Gamma=T_H(1-T_1)(1-T_2)\Gamma$ is — the grouping of intermediate $(1-T)$ factors of `prop:method-product` is necessary. `\uses{prop:method-product, lem:appN-algebra}`. Proof: full (algebra re-derived; (c) by the scaling at the collinear singularity). Pointers: `ex:disall-ladder` (Ch10) and `fig:disall-ladder-regions` (owner Ch10); three regions including the hard one (ERR-M11).
- **命题 `prop:appN-triangle-cs` — 单圈离壳三角图：纤维化 Collins 条件的完整验证与积分减除定理** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$：对此图在此阶严格；计算机辅助——非初等的不等式只有线性规划最优值，由精确有理原始–对偶对证明。）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Setting of `prop:appN-triangle-exact` (units $\tilde Q=1$, $P=P'=1/\sqrt2$, $u=k^+/P$, $\kappa=\mathbf k_T^2$, $s=\lambda^2=m^2$), in the $k^-$-residue representation of `def:appN-fibered`, on the natural cover $U_S=\{0<u\le s,\ \kappa\le us\}$, $U_{C_1}=\{s<u\le1,\ \kappa\le us\}\cup\{1<u\le2,\ \kappa\le s\}$, $U_{C_2}=\{0<u\le s,\ us<\kappa\le u\}\cup\{\text{sliver }-m^2/2P'<k^+<0\}$, $U_H$ = the complement (all UV directions and the interior of the Sudakov diamond). (a) *(fibered conditions)* (FR) holds for $\Gamma$ and every $C_R\Gamma$; at $\varepsilon=0$ every (CS1$^\varphi$)/(CS2$^\varphi$) item is $\le C\lambda^2L^{\le2}$; for $\varepsilon\in(0,1/10]$ every item is $\le C(\lambda^2L^2+\lambda^{8/5})$, uniformly; (CS3$^\varphi$) holds; hence (`thm:appN-fibered-subtraction`) $\int_B|\rho_r|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C\lambda^2L^2$ at $\varepsilon=0$ and $\le C(\lambda^2L^2+\lambda^{8/5})$ uniformly on $(0,1/10]$; the exponent is optimal among the covers $s=\lambda^\theta$, $\theta\in(0,2]$ ($\int_{U_S}\rho_{C_{C_1}}\ge s/4$). (b) *(identification of the Collins terms)* the fibered values of the four Collins terms equal their covariant (whole-integrand parametric) values — the closed forms of `prop:appN-triangle-exact`(b) — for $0<\varepsilon<1$, with no convention for scaleless integrals. (c) *(integrated statement; an implication)* if the fibered value of $\Gamma$ equals its covariant value $I_\Gamma=\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)\int_{\rm simplex}F^{-1-\varepsilon}$, $F=x_gm^2(x_1+x_2)+x_1x_2Q^2$, for $\varepsilon\in(-1,1)$ (main part ↔ $\{x_1>\tau x_2\}$, sliver ↔ $\{x_1<\tau x_2\}$, $\tau=m^2/\tilde Q^2$), then $|I_\Gamma-\sum_RI_{C_R\Gamma}|\le\int|\rho_r|\,d\mu_\varepsilon\le C(\lambda^2L^2+\lambda^{8/5})$ uniformly on $(0,1/10]$, i.e. `thm:appN-subtraction` for the triangle without a four-dimensional contour. (d) *(log-weighted family)* for $\Gamma^{(j)}=\ln^j(D_g/\tilde Q^2)\,\Gamma$ (hybrid scheme: outer measure in $d=4-2\varepsilon$ with a four-dimensional weight; a model of the insertion structure of a large-$\beta_0$ chain, **not** a graph class of (M1)) and each fixed $j$: every item and $\int|\rho^{(j)}_r|$ are $\le C_j\lambda^2L^{j+2}$ at $\varepsilon=0$ and $\le C_j(\lambda^2+\lambda^{8/5})L^{j+2}$ on $(0,1/10]$, with $C_j\gtrsim2j!/L_0^{j+2}$ (no statement about the summed family). `\uses{prop:appN-triangle-exact, def:appN-fibered, thm:appN-fibered-subtraction, lem:appN-lp-certificate}`. Proof: full, computer-assisted — closed-form fibered densities (single residues; symbolic and independent residue codes); exact rational primal–dual certificates on 58 piece records (546 monomial certificates; face bounds $\le2$; exact recession slopes $-1/2$ at $\varepsilon=0$ and $-9/20$ at $\varepsilon_0$), re-verified by independent code (`lem:appN-lp-certificate`(c)); uniformity from $\kappa^{-\varepsilon}\le1+\kappa^{-\varepsilon_0}$; (b) by explicit $\kappa$- and $x$-integrals (Beta functions) and the whole-integrand Feynman/Cheng–Wu representation; (c) from (a) and `thm:appN-fibered-subtraction`(iii) given its premise (spec note, not printed: the premise is X2 Prop B.3 — the main part and the sliver mapped onto the two simplex sub-regions, checked symbolically and numerically to $10^{-17}$–$10^{-28}$ (`r3_FI_exact.py`) and independently by the final review (`review_final_scripts/chk_B3.log`, $10^{-25}$); it becomes part of (b), and (c) unconditional with the status sentence of X2 §11, once a second independent confirmation is recorded or the lead author accepts the final review as sufficient; until then the book may quote the numerical agreement as evidence, [P]); (d) by a four-shell representation and an exact rectangle rule (2986 piece records, 70 373 certificates). Numerics [P]: $\int|\rho_r|\approx\lambda^2(4\tilde L-0.69)$, one logarithm more than the signed remainder $\int r=-4\lambda^2+O(\lambda^4\tilde L^2)$. Scripts and archives: `verify/extend/X2-subtraction/` (`r3_triangle_recert.py`, `r3_archive_verify.py`, `r3_FI_exact.py`, `r3_bubble_recert.py`). **Novelty:** 本书新结果（据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索：INSPIRE 与全文检索，"Collins subtraction" 与 tropical、Newton polytope、linear programming、integral norm、light-cone ordered、forest formula 等的组合）for (a) only — the complete verification of Collins' conditions in an integral norm on a real fibered base, with the optimal exponent and uniformly in $\varepsilon$ (confirmed by independent reviews: `verify/extend_reports/X2-subtraction-rev2-2.md`, `-rev3-1.md`, `-rev3-2.md`, archive re-verified by the final review, `verify/extend/X2-subtraction/review_final_scripts/rerun_r3_archive_verify_triangle.log`; literature check: X2 final report §12 and the queries of `-rev3-2.md`); the closed forms of the $C_R$ are the known region values (`Smirnov2002`, `Belitsky:2024yag`, `Belitsky:2025bez` App. A.1); (b)–(d) are not marked new. Slot X2a: delivered.
- **命题 `prop:appN-phi36-dis` — $\varphi^3_6$ 中 DIS 前向振幅：全部单圈图与单梯级梯形图** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$：对所列图在所列阶严格。）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Model (M2): $\varphi^3_6$ with a complex "quark" $\psi$ and a real $\phi$, $d=6-2\varepsilon$, off-shell target $p^2=-m^2$, frame $q^+=0$, $Q^2=q_T^2$, $x\ge x_0>1$ (unphysical region, no cuts; $\lambda=m/Q$, $c_x=1-1/x$). (a) *(all one-loop graphs)* the forward amplitude is defined modulo $x$-independent terms (the $t$-channel graph is $\propto1/t$ at $t=0$ and has no discontinuity in $\omega$); the vertex and hard-line self-energy graphs, renormalized at the integrand level with subtraction points matching the plus components (explicit here; cf. `asm:appN-uv`(U1); $T_H$ commutes with the subtraction), satisfy (CS3$^\varphi$) uniformly in $\varepsilon$ with $\mathcal R=\{H\}$: vertex $\int|\rho|\le C(x)\lambda^2L$ (numerically $\approx(0.46L+3.1)\lambda^2$), self-energy $\le C(x)\lambda^2$ ($\approx0.871\lambda^2$); the $J$–$\phi$ mixing graphs ($q^+=0$ zero-mode loops, outside the fibered framework) have the exact remainder $[S(P)-S(\hat P)]D(q)\Pi_R(q^2)=O(\lambda^2)$; in QCD the analogues of the mixing and $t$-channel graphs vanish by colour. (b) *(one-rung ladder)* with $U_{R_1}=\{v'\le\lambda^2\}$ and $U_H$ the complement ($\lambda\le c_x/8$): $\int dz\,d^4k\,|\rho_r|\le C(x)\lambda$, all items $\le C(x)\lambda$, (CS3$^\varphi$) holds ($\rho_{C_{R_1}}$, $\rho_{C_H}$ integrable only for $\varepsilon>0$), and at $\varepsilon=0$ $\int|\rho_r|=c_1(x)\lambda+O(\lambda^2L)$ with $c_1(x)=4\int_0^1d\xi\,\xi^{1/2}(1-\xi)^{3/2}/(1-\xi/x)^2$ ($c_1(2)=1.262984$). (c) *(identification)* the fibered values of $\Gamma$ ($-1<\varepsilon<1$), of $C_{R_1}$ and of $C_H$ ($0<\varepsilon<1$) of the one-rung ladder equal their covariant values in closed form ($C_H$ as the continuation of the covariant value of the massless on-shell target ladder, i.e. its DR value with the scaleless term set to zero; the whole-integrand parametric variant of $C_H$ agrees numerically only, [P]). Ladders with $n\ge2$ rungs: numerical evidence only ([P]); slot X2c (the fermion two-rung ladder) was not delivered. `\uses{prop:appN-ladder, def:appN-fibered, thm:appN-fibered-subtraction}`. Proof: full for (a), (b) at $\varepsilon=0$ (hard-factor lemma $d\ge z\xi c_x>0$ by Cauchy–Schwarz; one-dimensional reduction of the rung kernels; the exact odd-part integral gives $c_1$); (c) by Feynman parameters, maps onto the covariant simplex and a continuation lemma for subtracted Mellin integrals; $\varepsilon$-uniformity of (b) sketched only. Scripts: `verify/extend/X2-subtraction/` (`r1_dis_Honly.py`, `r2_ladder1_even.py`, `r3_FI_exact.py`, `r3_ladder_CH_exact.py`). Elementary; not marked new ($c_1$ was first derived in an X2 review).
- **命题 `prop:appN-massive-photon` — 阿贝尔有质量光子：类光近似子的精确余项与快度正规子** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Model (M3): massless on-shell legs $p_1=(P,0,\mathbf 0_T)$, $p_2=(0,-P',\mathbf 0_T)$, exchanged boson of mass $M$, $\lambda^2=M^2/Q^2$, $Q^2=2PP'$. (a) *(light-like approximators)* $r=(1/G-1/g_0)\delta_1\delta_2=-M^2g_0/(Gt_1h_1t_2h_2)$ exactly; in the fibered form the $\kappa$-integral of the $0<u<1$ part vanishes identically, and $\int r=-\lambda^2(L+1)-\lambda^4(\frac L2+\frac14)+O(\lambda^6L)$ exactly (consistent with `Jantzen2011`); (CS3) fails for $C_S$, $C_{C_1}$, $C_{C_2}$ at every $\varepsilon$ (rapidity divergences), so `thm:appN-subtraction` does not apply although $r$ is integrable and $O(\lambda^2L)$: the remainder bound does not need (CS3), factorization into separately defined factors does. (b) *(time-like rapidity regulators)* $h_{1Y}=2P(k^-+e^{-2Y}k^+)$, $h_{2Y}=2P'(k^++e^{-2Y}k^-)$: no eikonal–photon pole collision on the real base and (CS3$^\varphi$) for $\varepsilon>0$; for $Y\ge L/2$ the remainder equals the light-like value within $O(\lambda^2)$ (window and norm $\approx2.9\lambda^2L$: [P]). (c) *(space-like rapidity regulators)* $h_{1Y}=2P(k^--e^{-2Y}k^+)-i0$, $h_{2Y}=2P'(k^+-e^{-2Y}k^-)-i0$: the four-dimensional integral has **no pinch** (Gram determinant $-16P^2P'^2(1-e^{-4Y})^2\ne0$); the real base carries three simple poles ($x=0$, a pinch of the fiber contour between the two eikonal poles; $x=-x_0'+i0$ and $x=x_0-i0$, photon–eikonal collisions), so the real-base integral exists as a distribution but not absolutely; these $i0$'s are compatible with the symmetric Glauber deformation only locally in the Glauber region and are not those of `Collins2011` eq. (10.68); which assignment gives universal soft factors in space-like form-factor kinematics is not settled. `\uses{def:appN-fibered, def:appN-tmd-approx}`. Proof: full for the exact formulas and the pole structure (X2 scripts `t13`, `t16`, `r2_spacelike_poles.py`). Known: rapidity divergences of light-like lines with a massive gauge boson and their cure by non-light-like lines (`Collins2011` §§10.5–10.6, `Collins:1999dz`, `Jantzen2011` §6, `ChiuEtAl2012a`, `BecherNeubert2011`). New (elementary): the exact light-like remainder formula, the remark that the remainder bound does not need (CS3), and the three-pole structure (the earlier "pinch" diagnosis is withdrawn).
- **命题 `prop:appN-twoloop-algebra` — 两圈平面顶点：区域代数与字面约定下 (Abs) 的失效** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Two-loop planar vertex of (M1) (inner rung $k_1$, outer rung $k_2$; the nine regions of `ex:appN-vertex-regions`; approximators = coordinate-zeroing sets on $(k_1^\pm,\mathbf k_{1T},k_2^\pm,\mathbf k_{2T})$ and the IR regulator). (a) With the **soft-first convention** (a jet line carrying soft momentum enters the hard factor with its soft-approximated momentum — "the hard factor does not depend on the soft momenta", `Collins2011` §10.6.6): the 12-edge Hasse diagram, meet closure, (Com), (Abs) for all 9 incomparable pairs, and chain recursion = product formula (`prop:method-product`) with 50 zeroing patterns hold exactly. (b) With the literal convention (the hard factor sees the light-cone projections of the exact jet momenta; soft momenta approximated only inside jet factors) the zeroing sets differ in four entries and (Abs) fails in exactly four cases — on $a_2$ for (C1C1, HS) and (C1C1, C2S), on $b_2$ for (C2C2, HS) and (C2C2, C1S). Soft-first is sufficient; necessity is not claimed. `\uses{ex:appN-vertex-regions, lem:appN-algebra}`. Proof: full (exact symbolic enumeration; X2 scripts `t8`, `r1_abs_naive.py`). It corrects the claim behind `lem:appN-algebra`(iii) that (Abs) always holds for per-line meets; the configuration differs from `rem:appN-abs-edge` (slot X2d, open).
- **假设 `asm:appN-analytic-reg` — 两圈图样积分的辅助解析正规化** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content (two-loop planar vertex of (M1), each line raised to an independent power $1+\delta_l$): **(RA1)** the analytically regularized pattern integrals are meromorphic in $(\varepsilon,\delta)$ (analytic renormalization proves this for quadratic propagators, not for the eikonal-type linear factors); **(RA2)** the nine single-approximator integrals $\int T_R\Gamma$ are regular at $\delta=0$ for $\varepsilon$ in a punctured neighbourhood of 0, and their $\delta\to0$ limits are their dimensionally regularized values. `\uses{prop:appN-twoloop-algebra}`.
- **命题 `prop:appN-twoloop-remainder` — 两圈平面顶点：Collins 项与区域积分的等同及积分余项** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-analytic-reg}、\cref{asm:appN-values}(V2)。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）In the setting of `prop:appN-twoloop-algebra` (soft-first): (i) *(scaling structure)* the 9 single-approximator terms $T_R\Gamma$ have a unique homogeneity scaling and $m^2$-exponents $-k_R\varepsilon$ ($k_R=0$ for HH, 1 for HC1, HC2, 2 for C1C1, C2C2, HS, 3 for C1S, C2S, 4 for SS); the 40 composite patterns are homogeneous under 1- to 4-parameter families, 22 of them with exact $\varepsilon$-independent ("rapidity-type") invariances, so "scaleless ⇒ 0 in DR" does not apply to these 22; with independent analytic powers the weight map is injective, so every composite is homogeneous under a scaling of non-zero weight; (ii) *(identification)* each $T_R\Gamma$ is the leading-power region integrand of `Smirnov:1998vk`, factor by factor, after a unit-Jacobian translation of the routing (dictionary HH↔(h-h), HC2↔(1c-h), HC1↔(2c-h), C2C2↔(1c-1c), C1C1↔(2c-2c), HS↔(us-h), C2S↔(us-1c), C1S↔(us-2c), SS↔(us-us)); (iii) $\int\Gamma-\int r=\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma=\sum_{\rm 9\ singles}\int T_R\Gamma$; (iv) hence, with the region values of `Smirnov:1998vk`, $\int r=\Phi^{(2)}(t,t)/Q^4-{\rm LP}$, ${\rm LP}=\tilde L^4/4+\pi^2\tilde L^2/2+7\pi^4/60$ ($\Phi^{(2)}$: `UsyukinaDavydychev1993a`), finite, and $\int r=-\lambda^2(\tilde L^4/2+\pi^2\tilde L^2+12+7\pi^4/30)+O(\lambda^4\tilde L^4)$; (v) if moreover the fibered and covariant values of $r$ agree (a further premise, supported by quasi-Monte-Carlo agreement), every fibered remainder norm is $\ge\frac12\lambda^2\tilde L^4(1+o(1))$. `\uses{asm:appN-analytic-reg, asm:appN-values, prop:appN-twoloop-algebra}`. Proof: (i), (ii) are unconditional and full (exact symbolic; X2 scripts `r1_twoloop_exact.py`, `r2_twoloop_regulator.py`, `r3_twoloop_regions.py`); (iii) DR linearity over the analytically continued pieces, (V2) for the composites and (RA2) for the singles; (iv) the printed region values of `Smirnov:1998vk` (summed independently to 90 digits) and the closed form of `UsyukinaDavydychev1993a`; (v) elementary. Without the region values a Vandermonde argument still gives that the grouped pole coefficients of order $\ge5$ vanish and that the finite part is a polynomial of degree $\le4$ in $\tilde L$. Positioning: `Smirnov:1998vk` treat exactly this diagram, its regions and the LP sum; `Belitsky:2025bez` App. A.2 give the nine integrals to $O(\varepsilon^2)$; the reduction of nested subtraction terms to region integrals is the zero-bin/overlap mechanism (`ManoharStewart2007`, `IdilbiMehen2007`, `Jantzen2011`). Ours (not marked new): the composite-pattern regulator analysis, the explicit identification at this order with the regulator requirements made explicit, the NLP coefficient. Error type: exact given the premises.
- **注 `rem:appN-m1-infrared` — 超可重整模型 (M1) 的红外结构：全阶例子需要的模型** (no tag). (M1) has $[g]$ = mass$^1$ in $d=4$ (super-renormalizable). (a) *Counterexample to "UV finite ⇒ (CS3$^\varphi$)":* the three-loop vertex graph obtained by inserting the massless two-loop "kite" self-energy into the exchanged $\phi$ line has no tadpole and no one-loop two-point subgraph, every subgraph is UV finite and every loop carries plus momentum, yet its integral diverges at the soft corner for every $\varepsilon\in[0,\varepsilon_0]$ (it converges only for $\varepsilon<-1/3$; the DR value continued from there is finite at $\varepsilon=0$). (b) Self-energy insertions on lines that can become soft make the off-shell form factor $F^{(N)}$ of (M1) IR divergent at fixed $m$ for $N\ge2$ — the infrared problem of super-renormalizable theories (`Jackiw:1980kv`). (c) *Soft power counting:* for a set of simultaneously soft lines with connected complement, with $V_S$ all-soft cubic vertices, $V_2$ two-point insertions of mass dimension two on soft lines and $n_a$ endpoints on non-soft vertices, the infrared degree is $\omega_{\rm IR}=n_a-V_S-2V_2$, and a divergence is expected for $\omega_{\rm IR}\le0$ (single soft $\phi$: $+2$; two soft rungs: $+4$; a one-loop $\psi$-bubble or a $\psi$ self-energy on a soft line: $0$; the kite on a soft $\phi$: $-2$; a soft $\psi$ triangle or box with soft $\phi$ legs: $0$); "no self-energy subgraph" is not sufficient for IR finiteness in (M1). (d) In $\varphi^3_6$ ((M2)) $\omega_{\rm IR}=2n_a>0$: no soft IR problem. Consequence: an all-order statement about a physical off-shell form factor needs an IR-safe model (a renormalizable scalar theory, a massive $\phi$, or a gauge theory) — open problem (Ch25). Proofs: (a), (b) parametric exponents and explicit corner integrals (X2 script `r3_M1_infrared.py`); (c), (d) power counting, used as a heuristic.
- **注 `rem:appN-fibered-allorders` — 全阶的纤维化减除：条件形式、范围与定位** (no tag). (F.1) For (M1) at fixed order $N$ and a finite set of graphs whose **renormalized** integrands (integrand-level renormalization of the UV-divergent subgraphs — in an IR-finite (M1) graph these are $J$–$\phi$-type two-point insertions on lines of fixed momentum) satisfy (IRF), with regions = the meet closure of the leading regions and soft-first approximators: if (FR) and (FI) hold for every graph and Collins term, a cover with (CS1$^\varphi$)/(CS2$^\varphi$) exists and (CS3$^\varphi$) holds, then the sum of the Collins terms equals the sum of the graphs up to $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ uniformly in $\varepsilon$ (`thm:appN-fibered-subtraction`(iii), summed), and if the $I_{C_R\Gamma}$ are meromorphic at $\varepsilon=0$ the UV poles of single terms cancel (`lem:method-no-pole`). Verified premises: only at $N=1$ — the triangle (`prop:appN-triangle-cs`) and the $J$–$\phi$ graph, whose remainder $\Pi_R(q^2)D(q)-\Pi_R(\hat q^2)D(\hat q)=O(\lambda^2)$ is computed exactly; at $N=2$ the algebra (`prop:appN-twoloop-algebra`) and the integrated planar remainder (`prop:appN-twoloop-remainder`, conditional); not verified: (FR)+(FI) of the Collins terms at $N\ge2$ (22 rapidity-type composites), covers for the two-loop vertices, gauge numerators and Ward identities (`asm:appN-ward-product`), slot X2d. (F.2) The full form factor of (M1) is IR divergent for $N\ge2$ (`rem:appN-m1-infrared`): (F.1) concerns IR-finite graph classes, not the physical form factor. (F.3) Subtraction is not factorization off shell: `Belitsky:2025bez` find that hard factorization holds to three loops in the off-shell Sudakov form factor while the ultrasoft and collinear modes do not separate (an "entangling" term, their eq. (6.18), in the present language the leading term of $T_{C1S}$ that keeps $2(P-k_1^+)k_2^-$); the factorized form needs in addition the Ward-identity/Wilson-line structure. (F.4) Positioning: nested subtractions at the level of pinch surfaces (`Collins2011` ch. 10, `Erdogan:2014gha`, `Ma2020`, `FeigeSchwartz2014`, `Anastasiou:2018rib`); the integrated leading-power region expansion is covered at the level of the method-of-regions literature (`Bergere:1977wz`, `BenekeSmirnov1998`, `Smirnov:1998vk`, `Jantzen2011`, `Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw`); open is the off-shell, integrand-level version for general graphs — integral-norm bounds with explicit power suppression, uniform in $\varepsilon$, together with the fibered–covariant identification — which X2 supplies for the graphs of `prop:appN-triangle-cs` and `prop:appN-phi36-dis`.

# PART 1c — Global rules for the appendices M–Q (verbatim)

## Global rules for the appendices M–Q (the former appendix-draft §00; binding for App M–Q)

### 00.1 Units, files, budgets (lead-author decision)

| unit | key | file | 中文标题 (binding) | pp | writer |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M | `appM` | `appM_ope.tex` | OPE：微扰证明、无质量 QCD、非微扰条件定理 | 24 | one writer (recommended: the Ch7 `dis` writer, after Ch7 statements are frozen) |
| N | `appN` | `appN_subtraction.tex` | Collins 减除法的精确形式 | 24 | one writer (recommended: also writes Ch9 `sec:method-subtraction`) — written **first** among M/N/O |
| O | `appO` | `appO_glauber.tex` | 光锥排序微扰论与 Drell–Yan 的 Glauber 相消 | 32 | one writer after the App N labels of §N.3–§N.5 are frozen (recommended: also writes Ch12 `sec:dy-glauber`, `sec:dy-model`, `sec:dy-theorem`) |
| P | `appP` | `appP_blueprint.tex` | 形式化蓝图：假设登记表、依赖图与状态表——由 \uses 数据自动生成 + 导言 | 10 | introduction by a writer (≈2 pp, last); tables and figures generated by the integrator |
| Q | `appQ` | `appQ_errata.tex` | 经核实的文献勘误 | 4 | written after `verify/` completes |

Labels: appendix `\label{app:appM}` … `\label{app:appQ}`; sections `sec:appM-…`; statements `def|asm|lem|prop|thm|cor|ex|rem:appM-…`; tables `tab:appM-…`; figures as in FIGURES_INDEX_v2. Chapter-owned statements whose proofs are delivered here keep their chapter labels (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §5.3, §6.3); they are listed in the **proof-delivery table** of each appendix and are **not** in the registry of the appendix.

### 00.2 How each entry is written (in addition to WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4, §6)

1. **Entry format.** `**类型 `label` — 中文标题** [tag, qualifier]` · premise sentence (R_cond) · partonic/non-perturbative line · *Statement* (formulas in the binding conventions) · `\uses{…}` · *Proof plan* (full / sketch; numbered steps; literature with location; checks with script paths) · *VF* IDs · *Error type* of the conclusion (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.7) · *Surpass/correction* note (ERR IDs refer to `refdoc/REFDOC_ANALYSIS.md` §2; never printed).
2. **Partonic line.** Every App N and App O statement concerns partonic integrands order by order; its hypotheses end with 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」. App M §M.2–§M.7 likewise (massive models, partonic Green functions). App M §M.8 is hadronic: its R_cond statements list `asm:intro-*` in the premise sentence and end with 「非微扰地位：见前提（MA0、MA3、MA4）。」.
3. **Qualifier sentences** are the verbatim strings of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3; the R_cond premise sentence 「前提：\cref{asm:…}、…」 lists every assumption and every non-[R] statement used, and the same labels are in `\uses`.
4. **`\uses` order inside one appendix file.** A label may be used only after it is defined in the same file (check C4). The order of the entries below respects this; writers keep it. Appendix statements may use statements of any chapter and of other appendices (no cycles; App P checks).
5. **Chapter statements proved here.** The appendix section starts with 「\cref{…} 的证明」; the statement is not repeated as an environment. The restatement given in this outline (marked *Target*) is for the appendix writer only; if it differs from the chapter's wording in `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2`, the chapter wording governs and the writer reports the difference. The chapter statement's `\uses` must contain the appendix lemmas its proof uses; the column "appendix lemmas for the chapter `\uses`" of the proof-delivery tables gives them (for the merge into the core outline).
6. **The reference document** is never cited, quoted or named (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.10). Internal identifiers such as "Thm D.10", "Ass. E.33" appear in this outline only for traceability. Every adopted construction is re-derived and attributed to the primary literature (Zimmermann; Lowenstein–Zimmermann; Joglekar–Lee; Weinberg; Collins 2011; Collins–Soper–Sterman; Bodwin; Aybat–Sterman; Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer; Kogut–Soper; Veltman; Rothstein–Stewart; …) or presented as our derivation.
7. **Error types.** Each status table (`tab:appM-status`, `tab:appN-status`, `tab:appO-checklist`) has the column 「误差类型」 with E-S / E-P / E-F / E-N / E-N$^\varphi$ (fibered integral norm, `def:appN-fibered`; v2.3) / E-W, and each `proofsketch` step that rests on power counting names its type.
8. **Box** 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」 right after the first paragraph of App M, N, O (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.6 frame; labels only). Drafts are given below.
9. **Conventions.** Light-cone vectors $n_A=(1^+,0^-,\mathbf 0_T)$, $n_B=(0^+,1^-,\mathbf 0_T)$ (row 2), never $n,\bar n$ (the reference's $n,\bar n$ are our $n_A,n_B$). Projections $\pi_+(k)=(n_B\cdot k)\,n_A=(k^+,0,\mathbf 0_T)$, $\pi_-(k)=(n_A\cdot k)\,n_B=(0,k^-,\mathbf 0_T)$. Glauber direction $e_G=n_B-n_A$ (row 38, VF-REF-7). Eikonal rules VF-WL-1 (row 28): past-pointing $W[0,-\infty n]$: $\prod_j\frac{i}{n\cdot(k_1+\dots+k_j)+i0}$; future-pointing $W[\infty n,0]$: $\prod_j\frac{-i}{n\cdot(k_j+\dots+k_m)-i0}$. Coupling $D_\mu=\partial_\mu-igA_\mu^at^a$, quark–gluon vertex $+ig\gamma^\mu t^a$ (the reference's "$-igJ^{+a}$" is a sign slip, ERR-m26).

### 00.3 Tag policy for the technical assumptions (consistency with the grade-propagation rule)

The grade-propagation rule (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.6) makes every process theorem carry at most the weakest tag of the technical assumptions it uses. The binding master-table tags of the collinear and TMD process theorems are [A]. Therefore (details and justification per entry in `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §00.3):
- a technical assumption that encodes a step of an **accepted all-order argument** of the literature (CSS, Bodwin, Collins 2011, Sterman) which has not been written in the precise form required here is tagged `\tagA`, and its 「地位」 field says "open in the precise form stated; covered at the level of the standard all-order argument by …" — this applies to all `asm:appM-*`, all `asm:appN-*` except `asm:appN-values` (convention, no tag), `asm:appN-ir-regulator` (`\tagC`) and `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` (`\tagC`, named gap, merge decision MD1), and all `asm:appO-*` except `asm:appO-adjacent-swap` and `asm:appO-real-jets` (`\tagP`: proven in the scalar spectator model, argued for QCD jets; they enter only the QCD transfer of the App O model theorems, which are [P], and are premises of no [A] statement; v2.3);
- an assumption that is **not** covered by any accepted argument is tagged `\tagC` (or `\tagP` if partly proven) and is never a premise of an [A] statement: `asm:intro-*` (global non-perturbative, excluded from propagation for ordinary statements), `asm:appN-ir-regulator`, the hadronic LaMET hypotheses, the non-perturbative analyticity hypotheses of the Liu programme;
- the register of REFDOC §5.3 is refined accordingly: `(Z2)` is split off as `asm:appN-ir-regulator`; the non-local BRST classification assumed by Collins 2011 §11.3.2 becomes the explicit entry `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` — `\tagC` at the merge (MD1: no all-order argument; frontier/others §8.3 assesses the general multilocal case as [C]); a premise only of `thm:appN-structure` and a named gap of the chapter [A] statements.

### 00.4 Outcomes of the proof extensions X1, X2, X3 (verify patch v2.3)

The verification workflow (`verify/`) ran three proof extensions: **X1 Glauber** (App O), **X2 subtraction** (App N), **X3 OPE** (App M); final reports `verify/extend_reports/X1-glauber_final.md`, `X2-subtraction_final.md`, `X3-ope_final.md` (each with an independent second review). Decision rules (applied):
- **PASS** = two independent confirmations recorded in `verify/` (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.5 item 2) and, for a novelty claim, a literature check: the statement is inserted with its tag, the dependent status lines, the assumption register and `tab:appX-status`/App P are updated.
- **PARTIAL** = the extension holds in a restricted setting: the statement is inserted with the restriction in its first sentence and tag [P] (or [R, R_PT] for an explicit fixed-order statement whose first sentence states the order; a model theorem is [P] as a QCD statement, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4).
- **FAIL / not delivered** = the reserved slot label is withdrawn (named below only by its slot ID; §10), the fallback stays, no claim is printed.

A slip in our own analysis is corrected here as well: REFDOC S48 states that in `thm:appO-main` "every soft attachment to $J_A$ ($J_B$) is replaced by a past-pointing Wilson line along $n_B$ ($n_A$)". Correct (eikonal factor $n_A^\mu/(n_A\cdot k)$ for a soft gluon on an $A$-collinear line): soft attachments to $J_A$ ($J_B$) give past-pointing lines along $n_A$ ($n_B$) **in the soft factor**, and $A$-collinear longitudinal gluons attached to $H$ or $J_B$ give the past-pointing line along $n_B$ **in the collinear factor of $A$** (c_glauber §1.5 Steps 1–2). The statements below use the correct form.
